TL;DR: TailProp 提出跨体制自适应传播的分层视觉骨干，用高斯与柯西稳定过程传播子作为互补基，通过内容条件的通道系数自适应融合，在多个视觉任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学启发的视觉传播模型通常在单一动力学族内构建和自适应传播，但视觉表示在不同样本、通道和网络阶段可能需要差异显著的空间交互，单一体制难以满足跨样本与跨层级的多样化需求。

Method: 提出 Tail Propagation Operator (TPO)：以高斯（快速衰减）与柯西（重尾）稳定过程传播子为互补基，预测内容条件化的通道共享系数进行自适应组合；由于系数空间共享，两响应可在 DCT 域用单对 DCT/IDCT 直接融合，实现方形特征图 N=HW、固定通道宽度下 O(N^1.5) 的空间混合；据此构建分层骨干 TailProp。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、语义分割、鲁棒性和跨骨干复原任务上一致优于匹配的传播基线；TailProp-B 在 ImageNet-1K 达 84.4% Top-1，3x Mask R-CNN 下 50.3/44.8 box/mask AP，ADE20K 达 50.8% mIoU。消融表明增益不能由单基传播、同族额外分支或族内自适应阶数单独解释。

Conclusion: 互补双基传播是一种有效的视觉表示学习设计原则，跨体制自适应传播相比单族传播具有更好的结构化与可解释性优势。

Abstract: Science-inspired vision models show that explicit propagation dynamics can provide structured and interpretable alternatives to conventional token mixing. Existing formulations, however, typically construct and adapt visual propagation within a particular dynamical family, while visual representations can require substantially different spatial interactions across samples, channels, and network stages. We explore cross-regime adaptive propagation and introduce TailProp, a hierarchical vision backbone built upon the Tail Propagation Operator (TPO). TPO uses Gaussian and Cauchy stable-process propagators as complementary bases with rapidly decaying and heavy-tailed spatial influence, and predicts a content-conditioned channel-wise coefficient to adaptively combine them. Because this coefficient is spatially shared, the two responses are fused directly in the DCT domain with a single DCT/IDCT pair, yielding $O(N^{1.5})$ spatial mixing for square feature maps with $N=HW$ and fixed channel width. Across image classification, object detection, semantic segmentation, robustness, and cross-backbone restoration, TailProp consistently outperforms matched propagation baselines; TailProp-B reaches 84.4% Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K, 50.3/44.8 box/mask AP under the 3x Mask R-CNN schedule, and 50.8% mIoU on ADE20K. Controlled ablations further show that these gains are not explained by single-basis propagation, an additional same-family branch, or within-family adaptive order alone, supporting complementary two-basis propagation as an effective design principle for visual representation learning.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Benchmarks: Using VLMs to Reveal Systematic Classification Failures Under Real World Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11126)
*Dieuwertje Alblas,Alma M. Liezenga,Jan Erik van Woerden,Fedor Taggenbrock,Dalia Aljawaheri,Klamer Schutte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文初步评估了基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的错误切片检测（ESD）方法在国防领域分类模型验证与确认（V&V）中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类模型的V&V目前依赖耗时的人工检查错误样本来发现系统性模式。VLM可将图像嵌入语义向量表示，从中提取可解释的系统性错误，有望加速这一流程。但国防场景面临两大挑战：(1)国防域在VLM训练数据中代表性不足；(2)周边环境与上下文多样性低于其他领域。

Method: 提出一种基于VLM的错误切片检测（ESD）方法，独立地对分类模型的系统性错误进行分组和标注。

Result: 在非军事数据集上，该方法能识别出具有作战相关性的、人为添加的扰动；在军事场景中，该方法能基于周边环境对图像进行聚类和描述，但聚类描述之间存在重叠。此外还研究了军事与非军事数据集在嵌入变异上的差异，该问题仍有待深入。

Conclusion: 结果尚不足以支持完全自动化的VLM-based ESD验证流程，但表明VLM未来可用于加速V&V过程。

Abstract: Verification and validation (V&V) of classification models is crucial to enable a wide range of sensor processing applications. Currently, the V&V process relies on time-consuming manual inspection of erroneous samples to find meaningful patterns. This work explores the use of Vision Language Models (VLMs) to speed up this laborious process. VLMs are trained to embed images into a semantically meaningful vector representation, from which human-interpretable systematic errors can be distilled. Deploying such VLM-based methods in a defence context introduces two major challenges: (1) the defence domain is underrepresented in the training data of VLMs, and (2) surroundings and context are less diverse than for other domains. This study provides an initial assessment of the suitability of VLM-based methods for V&V of defence applications. We propose a VLM-based error slice detection (ESD) method that independently groups and labels systematic errors made by a classification model. We demonstrate that this method is able to identify operationally-relevant artificially added perturbations in a non-military dataset. In a military context, our method clusters and describes images based on their surroundings, but also exhibits overlap between cluster descriptions. We further investigate the difference in embedding variation between our military and non-military dataset, which remains a topic of interest. Although the results do not yet warrant fully automated V&V through VLM-based ESD, they show that VLMs could be used to accelerate V&V processes in the future.

</details>


### [100] [ReconPlusGen: Injecting Reconstruction Prior into Multi-view 3D Generation through Noise Inversion and Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11129)
*Jiarui Liu,Heng Li,Weiyu Li,Keng Deng,Junyuan Deng,Zheng Zhongxing,Junyu Huang,Jiahao Chang,Xiaoguang Han,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出了重建引导的噪声初始化与调制机制：先用多张输入图像预测规范空间点云，再通过噪声反演将预测几何确定性地注入扩散过程，并对所得噪声进行调制，以兼顾几何保真与生成灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图三维重建需要同时满足两点：对可见区域几何的精确还原，以及对未观测区域的合理补全。纯重建方法在遮挡区域易产生缺失或伪影，而纯生成式扩散方法又难以严格对齐输入观测。因此需要一种既能确定性注入已知几何、又能保留生成灵活性的机制。

Method: 给定多张输入图像，模型首先预测规范空间（canonical space）中的点云。随后通过噪声反演（noise inversion）将预测几何确定性地注入扩散过程，并对反演得到的噪声进行调制，使其在保留生成多样性的同时约束可见几何。

Result: 在基准图像与真实世界图像上均取得了重建结果（定性结果），验证了该方法在合成与真实场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该核心思想通过“重建引导的噪声初始化 + 噪声调制”，在扩散框架内实现了观测几何的确定性注入与未观测区域生成式补全的统一。

Abstract: Qualitative results and an illustration of our core idea. Top left: reconstruction results on benchmark images. Top right: reconstruction results on real-world images. Bottom: illustration of reconstruction-guided noise initialization and modulation. Given multiple input images, we predict a point cloud in canonical space, deterministically inject the predicted geometry into the diffusion process through noise inversion, and modulate the resulting noise to preserve the generative flexibility required to complete unobserved regions and refine visible geometry.

</details>


### [101] [LAION-Mobile: Evaluating Deepfake Detectors On One Million Smartphone Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11134)
*Achim von Stryk,Janis Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LAION-Mobile 数据集（约 100 万张带 EXIF 的智能手机图像，源自 re-LAION-5B），并在 9,115 张评测样本上重新评估 12 个 SOTA 深度伪造检测器，揭示其在现代计算摄影图像上性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在各自基准上 AUC 近乎完美，但近期 ICML position paper 指出这些评测忽略了现代智能手机摄影的影响：设备端神经 ISP 流水线（多传感器融合、噪声与运动模糊抑制）使成像从简单镜头投影转向计算摄影，即"生成"而非"记录"照片。这可能使检测器将普通手机照片误判为伪造。由于缺乏大规模现代手机图像数据集，该假设此前仅在小规模概念验证中测试，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 LAION-Mobile 开放数据集（约 100 万张智能手机图像，含 EXIF 元数据，从 re-LAION-5B 蒸馏而来）；选取 12 个 SOTA 深度伪造检测器，使用其原论文 checkpoint，在 9,115 张图像的评测样本上进行评估（DIRE 使用 738 张）；分析阈值校准对误报率的影响。

Result: 三项关键发现：(i) 在现代 AI 内容上，无检测器 AUC 超过 0.624，12 个中有 5 个低于随机水平；(ii) 真实照片误报率是阈值校准的产物——在旧 GAN 数据上拟合的阈值使部分检测器看似可用（FPR < 11%），但按同一准则在现代内容上重拟合后，同样的检测器误报 17%–91% 的真实照片；(iii) 因此没有检测器能同时在现代 AI 内容上超越随机水平并保持可部署的真实照片误报率。语料覆盖第一代神经 ISP（2018–2020），当代旗舰机型基本缺失。

Conclusion: 现代计算摄影成像范式使现有深度伪造检测器普遍失效，真实照片误报率被旧的阈值校准所掩盖。LAION-Mobile 与 web 采集的设备构成一致，仅覆盖第一代神经 ISP（2018–2020），现代 ISP 时代仍是未填补的空白。

Abstract: Most Deepfake detectors report near-perfect AUC scores on their reference benchmarks. However, a recent ICML position paper argues that these evaluations collectively neglect the impact of modern smartphone photography: the widely used on-device neural image-signal processing pipelines (like multi-sensor fusion or noise and motion-blur suppression) increasingly shift the imaging paradigm from simple lens projections towards computational photography. Hence, devices actually generate, rather than record photos. This increases the risk that deepfake detectors may flag ordinary phone photos as fake. Due to the lack of large-scale datasets containing images from modern smartphones, this hypothesis has so far only been tested in small proof-of-concept studies. The aim of this paper is to close this gap. We introduce LAION-Mobile, an open dataset containing about 1 million smartphone images with EXIF metadata distilled from re-LAION-5B. Evaluating twelve state-of-the-art deepfake detectors with their original paper checkpoints on a 9,115-image evaluation sample of this pool (DIRE on 738), we report three key findings: (i) On modern AI content no detector exceeds AUC 0.624, and five of twelve fall below chance. (ii) Real-photo false-alarm rates are an artefact of threshold calibration: thresholds fitted on legacy GAN data make several detectors look deployable (less than 11 percent FPR), yet the same detectors flag 17-91 percent of real photos once the identical criterion is refit on modern content. (iii) Consequently, no detector both beats chance on modern AI content and keeps a deployable real-photo false-alarm rate. Mirroring the device mix of web collections, the corpus probes the first neural-ISP generation (2018-2020); current flagships are essentially absent, leaving the modern-ISP regime as the open gap.

</details>


### [102] [UniH$^3$: Unifying Hierarchical Homogeneity and Heterogeneity for All-in-One Medical Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11156)
*Zhiwen Yang,Jiayin Li,Chengyu Liu,Hui Zhang,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniH3 框架，通过统一层级同质性与异质性实现一体化医学图像复原，在 MedIR-2D-500K 与 MedIR-3D-3K 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一体化医学图像复原方法侧重建模任务间异质性（数据分布、退化类型差异），却忽视医学图像固有的同质性（跨模态/模态内共享的解剖结构），而该同质性可用于简化训练并提升泛化能力。

Method: 1) 层级同质性记忆（H2M）模块：训练时从高质量图像中逐步蒸馏任务内与任务间同质先验，并针对输入自适应检索最相关先验；2) 同质性引导注意力（HGA）：将检索到的先验高效注入复原流程；3) 层级异质性平衡器（H2B）：缓解优化中的任务间与任务内冲突，实现均衡有效的多任务学习。

Result: 在 MedIR-2D-500K 和 MedIR-3D-3K 两个大规模基准上，UniH3 在一体化与单任务医学图像复原中均取得最先进性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作为通用医学图像复原模型建立了强基准，并推动其发展；核心贡献在于同时统一了同质性与异质性的层级建模。

Abstract: All-in-One medical image restoration (MedIR) aims to address diverse tasks across modalities and degradation types using a single universal model. Existing methods typically prioritize modeling inter-task heterogeneity (e.g., distinct data distributions and degradation types). However, they largely neglect the inherent homogeneity present in medical images, such as widely shared anatomical structures within and across modalities, which can be leveraged to ease model training and improve generalization. To this end, we propose UniH3, a novel framework that Unifies Hierarchical Homogeneity and Heterogeneity for all-in-one medical image restoration. Specifically, to comprehensively exploit homogeneity, we introduce a Hierarchical Homogeneity Memory (H2M) module that progressively distills intra- and inter-task homogeneity priors from high-quality images during training, and adaptively retrieves the most relevant priors tailored to the input for guided restoration. These retrieved priors are then injected into the restoration pipeline via an efficient Homogeneity-Guided Attention (HGA) mechanism. Furthermore, to comprehensively address heterogeneity, we design a Hierarchical Heterogeneity Balancer (H2B) that mitigates both inter- and intra-task conflicts during optimization, facilitating balanced and effective multi-task learning. Extensive experiments on two large-scale benchmarks, MedIR-2D-500K and MedIR-3D-3K, demonstrate that UniH3 achieves state-of-the-art performance on both all-in-one and single-task medical image restoration. We hope this work establishes a strong benchmark and advances the development of general-purpose medical image restoration models. Code is available at https://github.com/Yaziwel/UniH3.

</details>


### [103] [Beyond Visual Quality: Evaluating Physical Consistency under Ego-Motion with EgoGenEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11172)
*Yilin Long,Chenming Zhu,Zitang Gou,Jingli Lin,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EgoGenEval——一个基于几何、无需位姿标注的基准，用于评估视觉生成器在自我运动下的物理一致性，涵盖单步与多步运动，分别度量相机运动落地（CMG）与场景状态保持（SSP）；并构建 EgoGen-Train 进行受控 SFT 研究，发现成对监督无法同时可靠提升两项能力，指向以轨迹为中心的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期视觉生成器虽能产出高保真图像，但在自我运动下常违反物理一致性，限制其用于空间推理与具身规划。现有基准多聚焦孤立图像或单步质量，该挑战仍未被充分探索。

Method: (1) 构建 EgoGenEval：几何锚定、无需位姿的基准，含 1,400 个案例与 2,360 个目标视角，覆盖单步与多步自我运动，分别以经盲评人类判断验证的 CMG 与 SSP 两项指标度量；评测 16 个无需位姿的生成器及 2 个位姿条件参考模型。(2) 用同一几何锚定流程构建 EgoGen-Train，开展受控 SFT 研究。

Result: 现有模型难以在执行相机运动的同时保持场景状态，无系统能同时在两个维度上表现良好。SFT 研究表明成对监督无法可靠地同时提升相机运动落地与场景状态保持：即便使用完整训练池与最长训练预算，场景保持的增益仅为相机运动增益的一小部分。

Conclusion: 成对、教师强制式目标本身是制约瓶颈，需转向以轨迹为中心的范式，将自条件 rollout 与显式位姿、可见性监督相结合。

Abstract: Recent visual generators produce high-fidelity images yet often violate physical consistency under ego-motion, limiting their use for spatial reasoning and embodied planning. Existing benchmarks largely focus on isolated images or single-step quality, leaving this challenge underexplored. We introduce EgoGenEval, a geometry-grounded, pose-free benchmark designed to evaluate the physical consistency of visual generators under ego-motion, and organize our study into two parts. (1) EgoGenEval contains 1,400 cases and 2,360 target views spanning single-step and multi-step ego-motion. It separately measures Camera Motion Grounding (CMG) and Scene State Preservation (SSP), with both metrics validated against blinded human judgments. Evaluating 16 pose-free generators together with two pose-conditioned references reveals that current models struggle to execute camera motion while maintaining scene state, and that no system performs well on both axes at once. (2) To examine whether benchmark-derived data can improve these capabilities, we build EgoGen-Train from the same geometry-grounded pipeline and run controlled SFT studies. These show that pairwise supervision does not reliably improve camera-motion grounding and scene-state preservation together: even at the full training pool and the longest budget, scene preservation gains a fraction of what camera motion does. This points to the pairwise teacher-forced objective itself as the binding constraint, motivating a trajectory-centric paradigm that couples self-conditioned rollouts with explicit pose and visibility supervision.

</details>


### [104] [A Multi-View and Confusion-Guided Ensemble Framework for Robust Synthetic Image Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11188)
*Zuomin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多视角、混淆引导的集成框架用于合成图像溯源(SIA)，结合FFT-ConvNeXt、DINOv2、CLIP与Xception捕获频域、语义与取证线索，并引入针对SD3/SD3.5的二元专家分类器与类别自适应置信度校准，公开榜99.53%、私有榜99.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着文生图模型快速发展，合成图像溯源(SIA)日益重要。但现代扩散生成器之间相似度不断提高，且实际图像常经历多样化后处理，导致准确识别生成源模型仍然困难。

Method: 构建多视角、混淆引导的集成框架：集成FFT-ConvNeXt、DINOv2、CLIP、Xception等互补架构，从频率、语义、取证多视角提取溯源线索；训练时采用模拟压缩、缩放、灰度化、模糊等真实后处理的强数据增强以提升鲁棒性；分析集成模型混淆模式，针对SD3与SD3.5的严重歧义引入低置信度条件下选择性激活的二元专家分类器；并采用类别自适应置信度校准提升难分类别（如Tencent Hunyuan）的区分度。

Result: 在DLMMDD Workshop @ ICANN 2026合成图像溯源挑战赛中，公开榜达到99.53%、私有榜达到99.20%。

Conclusion: 多视角集成结合混淆引导的专家分类器与类别自适应置信度校准，可有效缓解生成器间相似性与后处理带来的溯源难题；代码与实现细节已开源。

Abstract: Synthetic image attribution (SIA) has become increasingly important with the rapid advancement of text-to-image generation models. However, accurately identifying the source model of a generated image remains challenging due to the growing similarity among modern diffusion-based generators and the presence of diverse post-processing operations. In this report, we present a multi-view and confusion-guided ensemble framework for the Synthetic Image Attribution Challenge of the DLMMDD Workshop at ICANN 2026. Our approach integrates multiple complementary architectures, including FFT-ConvNeXt, DINOv2, CLIP, and Xception, to capture diverse attribution cues from frequency, semantic, and forensic perspectives. To improve robustness against unknown degradations and image manipulations, extensive data augmentation strategies are employed during training, simulating realistic post-processing operations such as compression, resizing, grayscale conversion, and blur. Furthermore, we analyze the confusion patterns of the ensemble model and observe severe ambiguity between Stable Diffusion 3 and Stable Diffusion 3.5. To address this issue, we introduce a dedicated binary expert classifier that is selectively activated under low-confidence conditions. We additionally apply class-adaptive confidence calibration to improve the discrimination of challenging classes such as Tencent Hunyuan. The proposed framework achieved 99.53% on the public leaderboard and 99.20% on the private leaderboard. The source code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/ZOMIN28/SIA.

</details>


### [105] [CEM-TUDASR: Computationally efficient multi-modality transformer based unsupervised domain adaptive super-resolution approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11201)
*Anjali Sarvaiya,Jay Kadel,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CEM-TUDASR——一种计算高效的无监督Transformer超分辨率框架，用于无线胶囊内镜（WCE）图像增强，无需配对LR-HR数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WCE可无创可视化胃肠道，但其微型光学、传感器限制与无线传输约束导致图像分辨率低，诊断重要结构的可见性下降，需要图像增强手段。

Method: 采用域自适应退化网络从常规内镜HR图像合成逼真的WCE类LR图像以缩小域差距，实现无配对学习；SR生成器集成深度注意力块（DAB）与融合注意力块（FAB），兼顾长程上下文依赖与局部细节；数据基于Kvasir Capsule构建，并在KID与GIANA上评估跨数据集泛化。

Result: 在BRISQUE、PIQE、NIQE及领域专用EndoQM等无参考指标上持续优于现有无监督SR方法；定性结果改善黏膜纹理、血管模式与临床相关解剖细节的恢复；视网膜图像跨域实验亦体现适应性；模型仅2.67M参数、169.94 GFLOPs。

Conclusion: CEM-TUDASR在保持计算高效的同时实现高质量重建，适用于资源受限的临床与嵌入式内镜应用。

Abstract: Wireless Capsule Endoscopy (WCE) enables non-invasive visualization of the gastrointestinal tract, but its miniaturized optics, sensor limitations, and wireless transmission constraints result in low-resolution images with reduced visibility of diagnostically important structures. This paper proposes CEM-TUDASR, a computationally efficient unsupervised Transformer-based super-resolution framework for WCE image enhancement without paired low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) training data. A domain-adaptive degradation network synthesizes realistic WCE-like LR images from HR conventional endoscopy images, reducing the domain gap and enabling effective unpaired learning. The SR generator integrates Deep Attention Blocks (DABs) and a Fusion Attention Block (FAB) to capture long-range contextual dependencies and fine local structures while preserving perceptual and structural fidelity. The model is trained on a curated dataset derived from Kvasir Capsule and evaluated on KID and GIANA for cross-dataset generalization. No-reference quality metrics, including BRISQUE, PIQE, NIQE, and the domain-specific EndoQM, show that CEM-TUDASR consistently outperforms existing unsupervised SR methods. Qualitative results further demonstrate improved restoration of mucosal textures, vascular patterns, and clinically relevant anatomical details. Cross-domain experiments on retinal images additionally demonstrate the adaptability of the framework. With only 2.67 million parameters and 169.94 GFLOPs, CEM-TUDASR achieves high-quality reconstruction while maintaining computational efficiency, making it suitable for resource-constrained clinical and embedded endoscopic applications.

</details>


### [106] [Tri-DehazeGS: Scene--Medium Decoupled Gaussian Splatting with Transmittance-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11223)
*Kui Jiang,Yang Gu,Jiacheng Liu,Shiyu Liu,Youyu Chen,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tri-DehazeGS提出一种场景-介质解耦的3D高斯泼溅框架，用独立三平面场建模雾霾介质，并通过透射率梯度补偿提升去雾重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多视角雾霾图像恢复干净3D场景的难点在于：雾霾会衰减场景辐亮并引入大气散射。现有散射感知高斯泼溅方法虽引入物理雾霾模型，但通常在图像空间施加退化，或把介质相关变量绑定到高斯基元上，导致干净场景辐亮与大气效应相互纠缠；同时低透射率区域对高斯优化提供弱监督，使远处或浓雾区域重建不足。作者认为去雾下的干净重建需要同时满足场景-介质解耦和透射率感知的优化再平衡。

Method: 提出Tri-DehazeGS框架：用高斯基元表示干净场景，用独立的视角共享三平面（tri-plane）场建模参与介质，并通过物理散射模型合成雾霾观测，从而实现场景与介质的解耦。进一步引入介质解耦透射率梯度补偿（MD-TGC），在冻结介质后补偿被雾霾抑制的梯度，且不改变前向渲染。

Result: 在真实与合成雾霾基准上的实验表明，Tri-DehazeGS提升了干净新视角重建效果。代码已开源于https://github.com/aptx46/Tri-DehazeGS。

Conclusion: 通过场景-介质解耦表示与透射率感知的梯度补偿，可有效缓解雾霾引起的辐亮纠缠与弱监督问题，实现更优的干净新视角重建。

Abstract: Recovering clean 3D scenes from hazy multi-view images is challenging because haze attenuates scene radiance and introduces atmospheric scattering. Recent scattering-aware Gaussian Splatting methods introduce physical haze models into reconstruction, but they often apply degradation in image space or bind medium-related variables to Gaussian primitives, which can entangle clean scene radiance with atmospheric effects. Moreover, low-transmittance regions provide weakened supervision for Gaussian optimization, causing distant or dense-haze areas to be under-reconstructed. We argue that clean reconstruction under haze requires both scene--medium disentanglement and transmittance-aware optimization rebalancing. To this end, we propose Tri-DehazeGS, a scene--medium decoupled Gaussian Splatting framework. It represents the clean scene with Gaussian primitives, models the participating medium using an independent view-shared tri-plane field, and composes hazy observations through a physical scattering model. We further introduce Medium-Decoupled Transmittance Gradient Compensation (MD-TGC), which compensates haze-suppressed gradients after medium freezing without altering forward rendering. Experiments on real and synthetic haze benchmarks show that Tri-DehazeGS improves clean novel-view reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/aptx46/Tri-DehazeGS.

</details>


### [107] [When is Test-Time Adaptation Identifiable From Unlabeled Evidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11235)
*Kartik Jhawar,Lipo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时自适应(TTA)中选择最优更新策略的可识别性问题，证明即使选择器完美，若观测通道无法区分排名不同的部署，则可靠选择不可能；并在有限批次高斯TTA模型中给出精确边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法试图从无标签测试数据预测哪种自适应最有效，但忽略了一个前提问题：选择器所依据的证据是否包含足够信息来确定最优动作。若证据本身存在歧义，再强的选择器也无法做出可靠决策。

Method: 作者形式化定义了观测通道的歧义条件：若两个部署在观测通道下表现相同但其TTA排名不同，则无法可靠选择；更丰富的证据只有在消除相关歧义时才能恢复决策。并在有限批次高斯TTA模型中精确刻画该边界，分析"不更新"与"均值重中心化"的比较及临界位移随1/√n的收缩规律。

Result: 在高斯TTA模型中，小偏移下不更新优于均值重中心化，超过唯一临界偏移后重中心化占优，且该边界随样本量以1/√n收缩。在CIFAR-100-C和DomainNet-126上的公开基准实验显示，现代TTA方法存在相同的失效模式：仅改变部署结构即可逆转oracle动作，而全局顺序无关证据保持不变。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了区分两类失效模式的实用方法：一是选择器能力弱，二是信息通道本身无法支撑所需决策。这有助于避免将信息不足误判为选择器缺陷。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers many ways to update a deployed model without labels, but choosing the wrong update can make a strong source model worse. Recent methods therefore try to predict which adaptation will work from unlabeled test data. We ask a prior question: does the evidence given to the selector contain enough information to determine the best action at all? We show that this is not guaranteed, even with a perfect selector. If an observation channel makes two deployments look the same while their TTA rankings differ, reliable selection is impossible from that channel; richer evidence can restore the decision only when it resolves the relevant ambiguity. We make this boundary exact in a finite-batch Gaussian TTA model, where doing nothing beats mean recentering for small shifts, recentering wins beyond a unique critical shift, and the boundary shrinks as $1/\sqrt n$. Public benchmark studies on CIFAR-100-C and DomainNet-126 show the same failure mode with modern TTA methods: changing only deployment structure can reverse the oracle action while global order-blind evidence remains unchanged. The result is a practical way to separate two failure modes that are usually mixed together: a weak selector versus an information channel that cannot support the desired decision in the first place.

</details>


### [108] [HALDETECT at ImageEval 2026 Shared Tasks: Answer-First Contrastive Grounding with QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11236)
*Syed Mohaiminul Hoque,Md Sakhawat Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 HALDETECT 系统，用于 ImageEval 2026 英文幻觉检测任务（Task 1b）：给定一张图像与三条文化上合理的陈述，识别唯一有视觉依据的一条。最佳方案以 4-bit QLoRA 微调 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 并冻结视觉编码器，在 1,000 条测试集上取得 Contrastive Instability (CI) 0.035，在八支队伍中位列第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型容易流畅地"编造"视觉细节，即产生幻觉，这限制了其在细粒度图像解读场景中的部署。为此需要在图像与多条看似合理的陈述之间做出唯一正确的视觉接地判断，从而评估并缓解幻觉问题。

Method: 将任务建模为一个对比式（contrastive）二选一/多选一决策；先输出答案再给出解释；推理结构围绕颜色/纹理、形状/形态、上下文三个维度组织。最佳提交适配器采用 4-bit QLoRA 微调 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct，同时冻结视觉编码器。此外进行了开发期实验，包括答案顺序效应、适配与纯提示的对比、对已发布金标签的回顾性配对分析，以及对四种训练规模的重播种（reseeding）实验。

Result: 最佳适配器在 1,000 条测试集上取得 CI 0.035，队伍排名第三（共八支）。开发实验发现：答案顺序的影响可能大于模型规模；适配优于纯提示。对金标签的回顾性配对分析证实 QLoRA 相对最佳提示的增益，但未能确认 devtest 所选适配器与最佳测试适配器之间的微小差距；重播种实验表明表面上的数据规模缩放曲线在更换随机种子后并不成立。35 个残余错误主要属于文化上合理的功能、材料与识别类区分，朴素适配器投票无帮助。

Conclusion: 在该文化接地幻觉检测任务中，基于 QLoRA 的参数适配确实优于纯提示，但若干看似稳健的开发期结论（答案顺序、数据规模缩放、最佳适配器选择）在更严格的统计与重播种检验下并不稳固；残余错误集中在文化相关的功能、材料与识别层面，简单投票集成无效。

Abstract: Large multimodal models tend to hallucinate visual detail fluently, which limits their deployment for fine-grained interpretation. We present HALDETECT, our system for the English hallucination-detection track (Task 1b) of ImageEval 2026, in which a system must identify, from an image and three culturally plausible statements, the single visually grounded one. We frame the item as one contrastive decision, emit the answer before its explanation, and structure reasoning around colour/texture, shape/form, and context. Our best submitted adapter fine-tunes Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct with 4-bit QLoRA while freezing the vision encoder and reaches Contrastive Instability (CI) 0.035 on the 1,000-item test set; we placed third of eight teams. Development experiments show that answer order can matter more than model scale and that adaptation beats prompting alone. Retrospective paired analysis of the released gold labels confirms the QLoRA gain over the best prompt but not the small gap between the devtest-selected and best-test adapters, and reseeding all four training sizes shows that the apparent data-scaling curve does not survive a seed change. The 35 residual errors are culturally plausible function, material, and recognition distinctions; naive adapter voting does not help.

</details>


### [109] [SCINTILLA-SNN: A Spiking Multi-Scale Selective Aggregation Network for Perineural Invasion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11237)
*Youngung Han,Yului Jeong,Kyeonghun Kim,Dohyun Kweon,Suah Park,Hyunsu Go,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Jinyong Jun,Yunho Choe,Yunjin Seo,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCINTILLA-SNN脉冲神经网络，采用四级层次主干与多尺度脉冲聚合模块，实现胆管癌周围神经侵犯的无创术前预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胆管癌周围神经侵犯的术前预测具有临床价值，但MRI上相关线索细微、稀疏且局限于肿瘤边界。标准3D CNN与Transformer以密集或空间均匀方式处理体数据，会稀释这些细微证据，且在3D特征网格上需大量乘累加运算。

Method: 构建含四级层次主干的3D脉冲网络，通过脉冲卷积阶段与局部脉冲窗口调制阶段提取层次化体表示；设计多尺度脉冲聚合（MSSA）模块，将每个空间token映射为可学习的内容值，并用由发放率与逐时间步膜电位变异性导出的脉冲动态门进行调制，得到诊断token分数以选择性聚合稀疏的PNI证据。

Result: 在182例胆管癌患者、跨度10年的回顾性队列上，5折交叉验证下AUROC达0.748，同时相比同网络纯密集MAC计算，估计推理能耗降低23.18倍。

Conclusion: SCINTILLA-SNN通过脉冲机制与稀疏证据的定向聚合，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时显著降低推理能耗，为MRI上细微、局部化病灶线索的术前预测提供了能效友好的方案。

Abstract: Preoperative prediction of perineural invasion (PNI) in cholangiocarcinoma (CCA) is clinically valuable but remains challenging because PNI-related cues on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) are subtle, sparse, and spatially localized around the tumor boundary. Standard 3D CNN and transformer architectures process volumetric data in a dense or spatially uniform manner, which can dilute subtle PNI-related evidence while requiring a large number of multiply-accumulate operations over 3D feature grids. To address these limitations, we propose SCINTILLA-SNN, a 3D spiking network composed of a four-stage hierarchical backbone and a Multi-Scale Spike Aggregation (MSSA) module for PNI prediction. The backbone extracts hierarchical volumetric representations through spiking convolutional stages and local spike window modulation stages. Given the resulting stage-wise representations, MSSA maps each spatial token to a learnable content value and modulates it with a spike-dynamics gate derived from firing rate and timestep-wise membrane-potential variability. The resulting score, referred to as the diagnostic token score, is used to selectively aggregate sparse PNI-related evidence. Experiments on a 10-year retrospective cohort of 182 CCA patients show that SCINTILLA-SNN achieves an AUROC of 0.748 under 5-fold cross-validation, while reducing the estimated inference energy by 23.18$\times$ compared with dense MAC-only computation of the same network.

</details>


### [110] [Fast and Accurate Monomodal 3D High Resolution Deep Registration of Drosophila Larval Brain Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11240)
*Daniel Reisenbüchler,Yousef Sadegheih,Michael Dittrich,Pratibha Kumari,Muhammad Usman,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源深度配准流程，将果蝇幼虫脑单次前向传播形变对齐到参考模板，精度、速度与鲁棒性均优于11种经典方法与7种学习方法基线，并发布代码与权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果蝇幼虫脑是神经科学紧凑模型系统，其遗传工具可在特定神经群体表达荧光标记，跨个体比较表达模式需将所有脑配准到共享解剖参考空间。现有流程主要基于经典配准方法，每个体积都需重新优化、常需逐例调参、单脑耗时达分钟级，难以作为常规预处理步骤。

Method: 训练一个深度配准网络，在单次前向传播中以高空间分辨率将幼虫脑形变对齐到参考模板，处理体积的体素数远高于通常报道的学习式3D配准规模；同时构建配套预处理流程与解剖锚定的评测流程。

Result: 在采用不同采集条件与质量分层的留出数据集上，与11种经典方法和7种学习方法基线比较，该流程最准确，解剖标志局部互信息较最强经典基线提升23个百分点；配准速度快1至2个数量级，且在采集质量下降时精度保持能力优于所有其他方法。

Conclusion: 该深度配准流程可作为高效的常规预处理步骤，并作为开源深度幼虫脑配准框架发布网络、训练权重与完整流程。

Abstract: The larval stage of Drosophila melanogaster is a compact model system for neuroscience whose genetic toolkit allows fluorescent markers to be expressed in defined neural populations, and comparing the resulting expression patterns across animals requires every brain to be registered into a shared anatomical reference space. Existing pipelines for this task are predominantly based on classical registration methods, which perform a new optimization for each volume, often require per-case parameter tuning, and can take minutes per brain, limiting their use as a routine preprocessing step. We present a trained deep registration pipeline that deformably aligns a larval brain to a reference template in a single forward pass at high spatial resolution, on volumes that hold several times more voxels than those learned 3D registration is normally reported on, together with the preprocessing and anatomy-anchored evaluation pipeline required to apply it. Against eleven classical and seven further learned baselines on a held-out collection acquired with different acquisition and quality strata, the proposed pipeline is the most accurate, improving on the strongest classical baseline by 23 percentage points of anatomical landmark-local mutual information. It registers a volume one to two orders of magnitude faster than the classical deformable pipelines, and it retains more of its accuracy than any other method as acquisition quality degrades. The network, its trained weights and the full pipeline are released as the open-source deep larval brain registration framework: https://github.com/agentdr1/deep-larval-brain-reg

</details>


### [111] [From Evaluation to Enhancement: Benchmarking and Improving Think-with-Video Reasoning for Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11242)
*Meng Luo,Yicheng Liu,Jiahao Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视频世界通用基准VWG-Bench及提示重写模型Vid-PRE，旨在评估并提升视频生成模型的"视频思考"能力（符号规则、物理规律、意图目标）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽视觉质量高，但能否真正"用视频思考"尚不明确；现有基准将视觉质量与认知正确性混为一谈，无法精确诊断推理能力缺陷。

Method: 1) VWG-Bench：覆盖9个推理维度、38个细粒度任务的综合基准；2) 三层VLM-as-Judge协议：分别评估视频级流畅度、任务级规则遵循、样本级目标达成；3) Vid-PRE：模型无关的提示重写器，将推理认知负担卸载给专用VLM，通过纯文本奖励的强化学习训练，生成简洁且约束感知的提示，避免视频级奖励信号的不稳定性。

Result: 领先模型渲染得分高，但在逻辑密集与规则约束任务上持续失败；Vid-PRE在多个生成器上显著提升推理表现，且无需修改架构。

Conclusion: VWG-Bench提供严格的诊断视角，Vid-PRE提供可扩展的路径，二者共同推动实现真正的"用视频思考"能力，数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Video generation has advanced to produce visually compelling and temporally coherent results. Yet, whether these models can genuinely think with video--executing symbolic rules, respecting physical laws, and pursuing intentional goals--remains an open question. Existing benchmarks only partially address this, often conflating visual quality with cognitive correctness. We introduce VWG-Bench (Video World Generalist Benchmark), a comprehensive benchmark spanning 9 reasoning dimensions and 38 fine-grained tasks. To enable precise diagnosis, we design a three-level VLM-as-Judge protocol that independently assesses video-level fluency, task-level rule adherence, and sample-level goal realization. Evaluations of leading models reveal a striking gap: while models achieve strong rendering scores, they consistently fail on logic-heavy and rule-constrained tasks. To address this, we propose Vid-PRE (Video Prompt Reasoner and Enhancer), a model-agnostic prompt rewriter that offloads the cognitive burden of reasoning to a dedicated VLM. Trained via reinforcement learning with purely text-based rewards, Vid-PRE produces concise, constraint-aware prompts without the instability of video-level reward signals. Experiments show that Vid-PRE yields substantial reasoning improvements across multiple generators without architectural modifications. Together, VWG-Bench and Vid-PRE offer a rigorous diagnostic lens and a scalable path toward true think-with-video capabilities. All data and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/KlingTeam/VWG-Bench.

</details>


### [112] [Uncertainty DMD: Restoring Diversity in Few-Step Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11265)
*Zixuan Duan,Xunzhi Xiang,Yabo Chen,Xin Zhang,Changhan Liu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Qi Fan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对DMD蒸馏自回归视频生成中多样性坍塌问题，提出Uncertainty DMD框架，通过在首块注入时间步扰动、后续块引入随机缓存写入来恢复随机性，无需改架构即可提升多样性与运动动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏可提升自回归（AR）视频生成效率，但会导致多样性坍塌：同一提示下不同噪声样本生成高度相似视频，运动动态减弱。作者旨在分析该退化的成因并加以解决。

Method: 作者将DMD蒸馏AR视频生成器中的退化归因于"结构化不确定性坍塌"：DMD的众数寻求偏差将不同噪声样本映射到近乎相同的首块，而确定性AR缓存将此坍塌状态传播至后续所有块，使根部的局部随机性损失演变为全局时间变化抑制。基于此提出Uncertainty DMD：首块时间步扰动以提升首块多样性，后续块随机缓存写入机制以在自回归条件中保留不确定性。训练与推理使用相同扰动机制，无需架构改动。

Result: 实验表明，Uncertainty DMD在保持每样本视觉质量相当的同时，持续提升多样性与运动动态。

Conclusion: Uncertainty DMD通过轻量级不确定性注入有效缓解DMD蒸馏AR视频生成的多样性坍塌，且不增加架构复杂度。

Abstract: Few-step distillation improves the efficiency of autoregressive (AR) video generation, but often causes diversity collapse: under the same prompt, different noise samples tend to produce highly similar videos with weakened motion dynamics. We analyze this degradation in Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD)-distilled AR video generators and find that, in the autoregressive setting, it takes the form of a structured uncertainty collapse: the mode-seeking bias of DMD maps different noise samples to nearly identical first chunks, and the deterministic AR cache then propagates this collapsed state to all subsequent chunks, turning a local loss of stochasticity at the rollout root into a global suppression of temporal variation. Based on this analysis, we propose Uncertainty DMD, a simple uncertainty-injection framework that restores stochasticity at two key stages of AR generation: a timestep perturbation for the first chunk to increase first-chunk diversity, and a stochastic cache-writing mechanism for later chunks to preserve uncertainty in autoregressive conditioning. The method requires no architectural changes and introduces only lightweight perturbation operations. The same perturbation mechanisms are used during both training and inference. Experiments show that Uncertainty DMD consistently improves diversity and motion dynamics while maintaining comparable per-sample visual quality.

</details>


### [113] [Improving Faint Object Detection for Space Situational Awareness with Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11269)
*Angela Cratere,Luca Ghilardi,Vishnu Reddy,Francesco Dell'Olio,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Roberto Furfaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度学习流水线，通过自动去星与背景重建来增强光学空间态势感知（SSA）图像中暗弱运动目标的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光学观测中检测低信噪比目标极具挑战，尤其在地月空间（X-GEO）环境中，结构化天空背景、密集星场和散射月光严重削弱了经典检测算法的性能。

Method: 该流水线将轻量级分割网络（Tiny-U-Net）用于生成恒星掩膜，并结合部分卷积变分自编码器（astro-VAE），学习天文背景的统计分布并对掩膜区域进行上下文感知修复；重建的背景图作为预处理步骤抑制固定源与背景不均匀性。作为概念验证，该方法与移位叠加（shift-and-stack）方案集成。

Result: 在针对X-GEO区域的真实地基望远镜观测数据上评估，结果表明该方法能高保真地重建无星背景，同时保留运动目标并显著提升可探测性。

Conclusion: 该方法为光学SSA场景中的暗弱运动目标检测提供了一种有效的数据驱动预处理策略。

Abstract: We present a deep-learning pipeline for enhancing the detection of faint moving objects in optical space situational awareness (SSA) imagery through automated star removal and background reconstruction. Detecting low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) objects remains extremely challenging in optical observations, particularly in the cislunar (X-GEO) environment, where structured sky backgrounds, dense stellar fields, and scattered moonlight significantly degrade the performance of classical detection algorithms. To address this problem, the proposed pipeline combines a lightweight segmentation network (Tiny-U-Net) to generate stellar masks with a partial-convolution variational autoencoder (astro-VAE), designed to learn the statistical distribution of astronomical backgrounds and perform context-aware inpainting of masked regions. The reconstructed background maps can then be used as a preprocessing step to suppress fixed sources and background inhomogeneities prior to detection. As a proof of concept, the approach is integrated with a shift-and-stack scheme and evaluated on real ground-based telescope observations targeting the X-GEO region. Results demonstrate that the method reconstructs star-free backgrounds with high fidelity, while preserving moving targets and significantly enhancing detectability, thereby providing an effective data-driven preprocessing strategy for faint moving-object detection in optical SSA scenarios.

</details>


### [114] [Order-Aware 2.5D Multiple Instance Learning for Preoperative MRI-Based Perineural Invasion Risk Assessment in Intrahepatic Cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11271)
*Hyunsu Go,Youngung Han,Kyeonghun Kim,Jinyong Jun,Junbeom Lee,Dohyun Kweon,Yului Jeong,Suah Park,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OAS-MIL弱监督框架，用有序2.5D slab序列预测肝内胆管癌神经侵犯，患者级五折交叉验证AUROC达0.770。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经侵犯（PNI）是肝内胆管癌（ICC）的不良组织病理学标志物，但通常仅能在术后切除标本中确认；术前T2加权MRI有望提供无创预测线索，然而标注仅在患者级可用，缺乏切片级或体素级标注。

Method: 提出Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning（OAS-MIL）：以肿瘤为中心的MRI裁剪区域表示为有序、相互重叠的2.5D slab序列（由相邻轴位切片构成）；共享编码器提取slab级特征，再由置换不变的集合注意力分支与双向序列注意力分支进行聚合。

Result: 在患者级采用五折标签分层交叉验证，OAS-MIL取得平均AUROC 0.770，优于所评估的体数据（volumetric）与MIL基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，轴位顺序为基于MRI的弱监督PNI预测提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is an adverse histopathologic marker in intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (ICC), but it is usually confirmed only after resection. Preoperative T2-weighted MRI may provide noninvasive imaging cues predictive of PNI, although labels are available only at the patient level without slice- or voxel-level annotations. We propose Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning (OAS-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level PNI prediction. Each tumor-centered MRI crop is represented as an ordered sequence of overlapping 2.5D slabs formed from contiguous axial slices. A shared encoder extracts slab-level features, which are aggregated by a permutation-invariant set-attention branch and a bidirectional sequence-attention branch. Using five-fold label-stratified cross-validation at the patient level, OAS-MIL achieved a mean AUROC of 0.770, outperforming the evaluated volumetric and MIL baselines. These results suggest that axial order provides a useful inductive bias for weakly supervised PNI prediction from MRI.

</details>


### [115] [SAMV-DUSt3R: Instance-Centric 3D Scene Decoupling from Sparse Multi-Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11279)
*Langxu Zhao,Zuan Gu,Yingdan Zhang,Pengfei Zhao,Tianhan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端模型SAMV-DUSt3R，将SAM2二维掩码注入MV-DUSt3R重建，通过Cross Flow Mask Block引导网络关注目标实例，并用Spatial RankGNN选择最优参考视角，实现物体级解耦与重建精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从三维场景中解耦出单个物体（实例级解耦）的需求日益增长，传统方法依赖多阶段流水线，难以同时保证形状精度与物体级分离。

Method: 在MV-DUSt3R重建框架中注入SAM2的二维掩码：(1) Cross Flow Mask Block利用掩码引导网络聚焦目标实例；(2) 轻量级Spatial RankGNN选择最优参考视角。整体为端到端结构，无需多阶段处理。

Result: Spatial RankGNN参考视角选择准确率达73.5%；与当前最先进基线相比，各项指标下平均重建精度提升约11%；表现出较强的实例解耦能力。

Conclusion: 该方法可实现端到端的实例级解耦重建，并在驾驶、机器人、AR/VR及文化遗产数字化等场景中具有明确应用价值。

Abstract: With the rising demand to decouple objects from 3D scenes, we propose SAMV-DUSt3R, an end-to-end model that injects SAM2 2D masks into MV-DUSt3R reconstruction. A Cross Flow Mask Block uses these masks to steer the network toward the target instance, jointly improving shape accuracy and achieving object-level disentanglement without multi-stage pipelines. To ensure reconstruction stability, a lightweight Spatial RankGNN selects the optimal reference view with a selection accuracy of 73.5\%. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method boosts average reconstruction precision by 11\% across various metrics compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results reveal a strong instance-disentanglement capability and clear benefits for driving, robotics, AR/VR, and heritage digitisation.

</details>


### [116] [Your Model Already Knows Don't Teach It, Learn to Ask It: Soft Prompting for Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11310)
*Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare,Siyi Li,Hewei Wang,Cesar Daniel Hernandez,Wei Zhao,Wolfgang M. Pauli,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在视觉-语言模型的小样本域外目标检测中，软提示（soft prompting）仅需1–3个可训练token即可匹敌最佳LoRA配置，且无灾难性遗忘，并能跨模型迁移与文本化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向航拍、工业、医疗等域外场景，仅用10张标注图像进行小样本目标检测。现有适配方案为离散提示优化与LoRA微调，本文重新考察第三条路径——软提示：冻结预训练骨干，仅优化少量连续提示token。

Method: 软提示微调，冻结预训练骨干。识别两个关键设计选择：(1) 将提示token置于视觉token与文本token之间的跨模态边界；(2) 用空空间token（empty space token）初始化提示。与离散提示优化、LoRA微调进行对照，并做参数效率、种子方差、遗忘、迁移、可文本化及跨任务扩展实验。

Result: 跨模态边界放置优于其他位置（10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP）；空空间token初始化优于语义与随机初始化。1–3个token（平均7,168参数）在Roboflow20-VL（14.2 mAP，10-shot）上匹敌最佳LoRA配置，训练参数少逾20,000倍。软提示优化更难、跨随机种子方差更高。但无遗忘：达到同等精度的LoRA使NaturalBench VQA精度相对下降35%，最大rank下达56%，而软提示保持预训练性能不变。学到的token表现为提示而非权重：无需重训即可迁移到Qwen3.5-9B（+0.8 mAP），并可文本化为可读提示，与DetPO持平、优于GEPA。在RoboCasa操作任务上，冻结的π_0.5视觉-语言-动作策略亦受益于软提示，token置于梯度瓶颈处时在三项任务中的两项匹敌LoRA基线。

Conclusion: 现代VLM已编码专门领域所需的大部分能力，瓶颈在于如何"提问"，即如何学习有效的提示。软提示是以极少参数量实现域外小样本适配、且避免遗忘的可行方案。

Abstract: We address few-shot object detection with vision-language models (VLMs) in out-of-domain settings such as aerial, industrial, and medical imagery, using only ten annotated images for supervision. Existing adaptation methods are discrete prompt optimization and LoRA fine-tuning. We revisit a third option: soft prompting, where a small number of continuous prompt tokens are optimized while the pretrained backbone remains frozen.
  We identify two key design choices. First, placing prompt tokens at the cross-modal boundary between visual and text tokens outperforms other placements (10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP). Second, initializing prompts from the empty space token outperforms semantic and random initialization.
  With these choices, one to three learned tokens (7,168 parameters on average) match the best LoRA configuration on Roboflow20-VL (14.2 mAP, 10-shot) while training over 20,000x fewer parameters. Soft prompting remains harder to optimize, exhibiting higher variance across random seeds. Unlike LoRA, however, it causes no forgetting: the LoRA rank matching our accuracy reduces NaturalBench VQA accuracy by 35% relative, rising to 56% at the largest rank, whereas soft prompting leaves pretrained performance unchanged.
  The learned tokens behave like prompts rather than weights. They transfer to a newer model without retraining (+0.8 mAP on Qwen3.5-9B) and can be verbalized into readable prompts competitive with prompt-search methods (matching DetPO and outperforming GEPA).
  The approach also extends beyond detection. On RoboCasa manipulation tasks, the frozen $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy benefits from soft prompting, matching the LoRA baseline on two of three tasks when tokens are placed at the gradient bottleneck. These results suggest modern VLMs already encode much of what is needed for specialized domains; the challenge is learning how to ask.

</details>


### [117] [GRIPNet: Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Guided Architecture for Pulmonary Nodule Detection in CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11312)
*Haojie Yang,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现肺结节呈高斯径向强度分布（径向R²>0.86），指出方形卷积与该径向信号不匹配，提出GRIPNet，用风车卷积、双频模块、空洞掩码注意力等模块对应强度分布特性，在三个公开基准上提升mAP@0.5至95.3%、91.6%、97.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌致死率高，低剂量CT筛查依赖早期小病灶检测，但小于6毫米的肺结节难以检出。现有方法将结节视为通用目标，忽略其成像物理特性。

Method: 先验证结节强度在几何中心达峰、呈高斯径向衰减，对三个公开基准的18,218个标注病灶拟合，各数据集及尺寸分层径向R²均值均>0.86。据此提出GRIPNet：风车卷积分解径向梯度、双频模块分离边界细节与结构上下文、空洞掩码注意力匹配衰减范围、自适应损失按显著性重加权样本。

Result: 在KanserSet、LUNA16、Lung-PET-CT-Dx上mAP@0.5分别达95.3%、91.6%、97.9%，并在实时速度下提升高IoU定位精度。

Conclusion: 结节外观具有高度规则的高斯径向结构，将检测器各模块与该强度分布的可测量属性对齐，可显著提升小结节检测与定位性能。

Abstract: Lung cancer causes more deaths than any other malignancy, and low-dose CT screening is the main pathway to early diagnosis. That pathway hinges on the smallest lesions, yet nodules below six millimeters remain hard to detect, because most methods treat a nodule as a generic object and ignore the imaging physics behind its appearance. We show that this appearance is highly regular. Intensity peaks at the geometric center of a nodule and decays radially in a Gaussian pattern, and a fit to 18,218 annotated lesions from three public benchmarks yields a mean radial coefficient of determination above 0.86 in every dataset and size stratum. A square convolution samples both axes uniformly and is mismatched to this radial signal, most severely for small nodules. Guided by this evidence, we propose GRIPNet (Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Network), a detector in which every module maps to a measurable property of the intensity distribution. Pinwheel convolutions decompose radial gradients, a dual-frequency module separates boundary detail from structural context, dilated masked attention matches the decay extent, and an adaptive loss reweights samples by conspicuity. GRIPNet raises mAP@0.5 to 95.3, 91.6 and 97.9 percent on KanserSet, LUNA16 and Lung-PET-CT-Dx while sharpening high-IoU localization at real-time speed.

</details>


### [118] [Mi-Ripple: Restoring Images Degraded by Iterative AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11317)
*Jiayin Chen,Yicheng Xu,Muting Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mi-Ripple 提出一种诊断引导的修复流程，用于抑制迭代参考条件图像编辑中出现的“数字波纹”，同时保护图像结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式参考条件图像编辑会引入网格状与颗粒状纹理，即“数字波纹”，影响生成图像质量；现有方法可能只优化频谱指标，忽视可见质量与结构保护。

Method: 将周期性晶格伪影与内容纠缠的颗粒纹理分离，结合选择性频谱陷波、结构感知平滑以及干净参考再生成，实现低失真滤波与视觉重建的权衡。

Result: 在十四次仅陷波执行中，全图残差标准差为 CIELAB 明度单位的 0.08–0.44；配对再生成示例中，参考清理使输出碎屑密度降低 45%。

Conclusion: Mi-Ripple 将可测量的伪影降低与视觉更干净的生成图像联系起来，而非仅优化频谱分数。

Abstract: Iterative reference-conditioned image editing can introduce grid-like and granular textures, commonly described as digital ripple. We present Mi-Ripple, a diagnosis-guided restoration workflow that suppresses this digital ripple while protecting image structure. Mi-Ripple separates periodic lattice artifacts from content-entangled granular texture, then combines selective spectral notching, structure-aware smoothing, and cleaned-reference regeneration. This separation enables low-distortion filtering when artifacts are spectrally isolated and visual reconstruction when filtering would erase legitimate detail. Across fourteen notch-only executions, whole-image residual standard deviation is 0.08--0.44 in CIELAB lightness units. In a paired regeneration example, reference cleaning reduces output debris density by 45\%. Mi-Ripple links measurable artifact reduction to visibly cleaner generated images, rather than optimizing a spectral score alone.

</details>


### [119] [Predictive Multi-Landmark OCT Tracking for Increased Motion Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11330)
*Konrad Reuter,Suresh Guttikonda,Chaitali Uday Karekar,Christian Betz,Alexander Schlaefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测性跟踪方法，通过在多个被跟踪标志点之间传播位置更新来获得全局位姿预测，从而提升OCT在高速运动下的跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学相干断层扫描（OCT）因高空间分辨率和固有深度感知能力，在无标记运动跟踪方面具有潜力。但现有OCT跟踪方法可跟踪的速度受限，尤其在为估计6D位姿而依次跟踪多个标志点时问题更为突出。

Method: 提出预测性跟踪方法：在多个被跟踪标志点之间传播位置更新，进而得到全局位姿预测，以应对高速运动场景。

Result: 在速度高达100 mm/s、连续跟踪多达9个标志点的条件下，RMSE低于1 mm。

Conclusion: 全局运动传播与预测能够有效提升OCT跟踪的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography is a promising modality for markerless motion tracking due to its high spatial resolution and inherent depth perception. However, existing OCT-based tracking approaches are limited in terms of trackable velocity, particularly when multiple landmarks are tracked sequentially for 6D pose estimation. In this work, we present a predictive tracking approach that propagates positional updates between multiple tracked landmarks to obtain a global pose prediction. This enables more robust tracking under high velocities. Our results demonstrate RMSEs below 1 mm for velocities up to 100 mm/s and up to nine consecutively tracked landmarks, highlighting the potential of global motion propagation and prediction for improving the robustness of OCT-based tracking.

</details>


### [120] [R4Tun: LLM-guided adaptive segmental tunnel lining segmentation in point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11360)
*Xinghui Tao,Zehao Ye,Guangming Wang,Jelena Ninić,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R4Tun：以LLM驱动的有界参数自适应框架，基于记忆、状态、知识三类结构化上下文扩展专家流水线SAM4Tun，实现管片隧道衬砌点云的自动化分割，并在30个Seg2Tunnel子集上验证精度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管片隧道衬砌的自动化检测需要从3D点云中自适应分割，但专家调参的流水线在隧道条件变化时性能退化；因此需要一种可控、无需标注、可跨LLM迁移的自适应机制。

Method: 在专家设计的SAM4Tun流水线上引入LLM驱动的有界参数调整，利用结构化的记忆(m)、状态(s)、知识(k)上下文引导调参；在30个Seg2Tunnel子集(13个规则、17个复杂)与3种LLM上进行评估，共270次运行(30隧道×3 LLM×3上下文设置)，并记录每次参数修改的理由以保证可审计性。

Result: 完整m+s+k设计将mIoU从0.18提升至0.43–0.48，OA从0.42提升至0.59–0.65；接近参考的规则(交错)子集mIoU达0.784–0.796。不同LLM呈现相似调参趋势(平均增益的95%置信区间重叠)，并一致调整共享的关键参数集。

Conclusion: R4Tun在测试的SAM4Tun–Seg2Tunnel场景中是一种可控、无标注、跨LLM一致的自适应机制，能稳定带来精度增益；本文将其定位为机制层面的贡献而非可直接部署的最终检测系统。

Abstract: Automated inspection of segmental tunnel linings requires adaptive segmentation from 3D point clouds, yet expert-tuned pipelines often degrade when tunnel conditions vary. This paper presents R4Tun, a large language model (LLM)-driven adaptation framework that extends an expert-designed pipeline (SAM4Tun) with bounded parameter tuning informed by structured context: memory ($m$), state ($s$), and knowledge ($k$). Evaluated on 30 selected Seg2Tunnel subsets (13 regular, 17 complex) across three LLMs, the full $m+s+k$ design raised mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) from 0.18 to 0.43--0.48 and overall accuracy (OA) from 0.42 to 0.59--0.65 relative to the static SAM4Tun baseline, with the near-reference regular (staggered) subsets reaching mIoU 0.784--0.796 across LLMs. Across 270 (30 tunnels $\times$ 3 different LLMs $\times$ 3 context settings) runs, the LLMs showed similar parameter-adjustment trends (with overlapping 95\% CIs on mean gains) and consistently adjusted a shared set of critical parameters. These results support R4Tun as a controlled, label-free, cross-LLM adaptation mechanism in the tested SAM4Tun--Seg2Tunnel setting, demonstrating consistent accuracy gains; we position R4Tun as a mechanism contribution rather than a deployable final-inspection system, in which each bounded parameter change is auditable via logged rationales.

</details>


### [121] [Vision Transformer-Based Multi-Level Feature Fusion for Multi-Label Sewer Defect Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11375)
*Xu Fang,Zhuoran Wang,Qing Li,Shengyu Zhang,Guanzhi Deng,Jianbiao He,Qingquan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出层级视觉Transformer模型Sewer-Transformer-ML及两个轻量化模型Sewer-MobileNet-ML、Sewer-Mobile-TransNet，用于下水道缺陷的多标签自动分类，在精度与计算复杂度间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下水道缺陷自动分类对基础设施状态评估与维护决策至关重要，但现有深度学习方法在大规模多标签场景下难以兼顾分类精度与计算复杂度。

Method: 构建带多级特征融合的层级视觉Transformer（Sewer-Transformer-ML），并设计两个面向资源受限巡检场景的轻量架构Sewer-MobileNet-ML与Sewer-Mobile-TransNet；同时开展预训练迁移与特征融合方式的消融实验。

Result: 在Sewer-ML测试集上，Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base取得F2_CIW 65.68%、F1_Normal 92.68%，位列公开排行榜第一，F2_CIW领先第二名7.6个百分点；Sewer-MobileNet-ML仅17M参数即达F2_CIW 65.73%，参数量较基础模型减少约95%；在Sewer-Capsule标准划分下Sewer-Mobile-TransNet分类准确率达96.43%；训练集缩减至1177张时，Sewer-ML预训练持续提升性能。消融表明Transformer特征适合直接拼接，注意力融合更利于多尺度CNN特征。

Conclusion: 研究为下水道自动巡检、轻量化模型设计及跨市政基础设施巡检平台的迁移适配提供了计算依据。

Abstract: Automated classification of sewer defects is essential for infrastructure condition assessment and maintenance decision-making, but existing deep learning methods struggle to balance classification accuracy and computational complexity in large-scale multi-label scenarios. This study develops Sewer-Transformer-ML, a hierarchical vision Transformer with multi-level feature fusion, together with two lightweight architectures, Sewer-MobileNet-ML and Sewer-Mobile-TransNet, for resource-constrained inspection scenarios. On the Sewer-ML test set, Sewer-Transformer-ML-Base achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.68% and an $F1_{\text{Normal}}$ of 92.68%, ranking first on the public leaderboard and exceeding the second-ranked method by 7.6 percentage points in $F2_{\text{CIW}}$. Sewer-MobileNet-ML achieved an $F2_{\text{CIW}}$ of 65.73% with only 17 M parameters, representing an approximately 95% parameter reduction relative to the base model. Under the standard Sewer-Capsule data split, Sewer-Mobile-TransNet achieved 96.43% classification accuracy. When the training set was reduced to 1,177 images, pretraining on Sewer-ML consistently improved model performance. Ablation experiments further showed that direct concatenation was more effective for Transformer features, whereas attention-based fusion better supported multiscale CNN features. These findings provide a computational basis for automated sewer inspection, lightweight model design, and adaptation across civil infrastructure inspection platforms.

</details>


### [122] [Brain-PACE: A Deep Siamese MRI Framework for Modelling Longitudinal Brain Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11378)
*Samuel Maddox,Jacob Newman,Saber Sami,Michal Mackiewicz,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,the Australian Imaging Biomarkers,Lifestyle flagship study of ageing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Brain-PACE框架，直接从配对T1加权MRI估计结构性脑衰老速度，在MCI人群中识别加速衰老并关联临床与tau负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑龄估计已成为评估脑健康与疾病的常用代理指标，但其纵向衰老轨迹尚不明确，临床实用性受限。

Method: 在已有Siamese纵向框架基础上开发Brain-PACE，直接从配对T1加权MRI估计结构性脑衰老速度；方法上扩展LILAC框架，结合空间注意力、软标签分布学习与Cramér距离目标函数，提升概率性能、降低预测偏倚并提供预测不确定性。

Result: 在42.6%的轻度认知障碍参与者中识别出加速衰老；更快的Brain-PACE与更严重的功能与认知损害相关（FAQ r=0.35，ADAS13 r=0.30，CDR-SB r=0.32），并与后扣带回（r=0.59）、楔前叶（r=0.47）和内嗅皮层（r=0.37）更大的区域tau负荷相关；这些关联强于由重复横断面脑龄估计间接计算的衰老速度。

Conclusion: 直接纵向建模可捕捉与持续病理变化相关的互补信息；Brain-PACE是一种对早期神经退行性变临床与生物学变化敏感的纵向影像表型。

Abstract: Brain age estimation has become a popular research proxy for assessing brain health and disease, yet longitudinal trajectories of brain ageing are still poorly defined, and clinical use is limited. Building on existing Siamese longitudinal frameworks, we develop Brain-Predicted Age Acceleration (Brain-PACE) to directly estimate the pace of structural brain ageing from paired T1-weighted MRI. Brain-PACE identified accelerated ageing in $42.6$% of participants with mild cognitive impairment. Faster Brain-PACE was associated with greater functional and cognitive impairment (FAQ; $r=0.35$, ADAS13; $r=0.30$, CDR-SB; $r=0.32$) and greater regional tau burden in the posterior cingulate ($r=0.59$), precuneus ($r=0.47$), and entorhinal cortex ($r=0.37$). These associations were stronger than those observed when pace was calculated indirectly from repeated cross-sectional brain age estimates, suggesting that direct longitudinal modelling captures complementary information relevant to ongoing pathological change. Methodologically, Brain-PACE extends the LILAC framework by combining spatial attention with soft label distribution learning and a Cramér distance objective, improving probabilistic performance and reducing prediction bias while providing measures of predictive uncertainty. Together, these findings support Brain-PACE as a complementary longitudinal imaging phenotype with sensitivity to relevant clinical and biological changes in early neurodegeneration.

</details>


### [123] [DINO-Med: A Unified Patch-Based Adaptation Framework for Multi-Modal Medical Image Analysis Applied to Liver Fibrosis Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11380)
*Boya Wang,Ruizhe Li,Chao Chen,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一patch级框架，将自然图像基础模型DINOv3适配多模态医学影像，以肝纤维化分期为案例，冻结DINOv3特征显著优于Radiomics、ResNet和SAM-Med2D基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然彩色图像与多通道医学扫描之间存在显著域差距，将DINOv3等自然图像基础模型直接适配多模态医学影像具有挑战性，需要一种高效统一的适配方案。

Method: 构建统一的patch级框架：无训练配准、自动定位、掩膜过滤patch提取，并用分层策略将patch级信息聚合为受试者级诊断。以肝纤维化分期为案例，对比四种patch特征表示（手工Radiomics、ResNet、SAM-Med2D、冻结DINOv3），统一使用轻量MLP头，并在刚性/形变配准两种设置下评估；训练仅用S1与S4两类。

Result: 基于CARE 2025 Liver Track 4队列360名受试者的10次随机90%/10%划分评估，DINOv3框架显著优于所有基线，S1分类准确率最高78.4%，S4为75.8%。

Conclusion: 冻结的DINOv3特征经统一patch级分层聚合，可有效弥合自然图像与多模态医学影像的域差距，单分类器同时支持明显纤维化检测与肝硬化分期。

Abstract: Adapting natural-image foundation models like DINOv3 to multi-modal medical imaging is challenging due to the significant domain gap between natural color images and multi-channel medical scans. We present a unified, patch-based framework that processes raw multimodal imaging through training-free registration, automated localization, and mask-filtered patch extraction. This architecture culminates in a hierarchical strategy that aggregates patch-level insights into subject-level diagnostics. Using liver fibrosis staging as a case study, we evaluate four patch-level feature representations: handcrafted Radiomics features, learned ResNet features, pre-trained foundation model SAM-Med2D features, and frozen DINOv3 features. To ensure a controlled comparison, all models utilize the same lightweight MLP head and are evaluated across both rigid and deformable registration settings. Our training protocol focuses on mild fibrosis (S1) and cirrhosis (S4) classes only, enabling a single classifier to address both substantial fibrosis detection and cirrhosis staging. Evaluated via 10 random train (90%)/ test (10%) splits on 360 subjects from the CARE 2025 Liver Track 4 cohort, our DINOv3-based framework significantly outperforms all baselines, achieving the best classification accuracy of 78.4% for S1 and 75.8% for S4.

</details>


### [124] [Multi-Modal Controlled Coherent Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11439)
*Yifei Liu,Qiong Cao,Hongwei Yi,Huaiguang Jiang,Changxing Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOCO——一个基于扩散的框架，可同时处理语音、文本描述与轨迹等多种同步输入，在无需对齐多模态数据的情况下生成连贯自然、协调同步的3D Avatar运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能自然地边行走边说话，但让3D Avatar在并发多模态输入（如描述行走的文本+语音音频）驱动下生成同样自然的运动十分困难。现有方法受限于对齐多模态数据的稀缺，通常按模态顺序组合或将各模态运动加权求和，往往导致运动不匹配或不真实。

Method: MOCO将运动生成过程解耦：在每一步去噪中，扩散模型从输入噪声独立地为每个模态生成运动，并按预定义的空间规则组装身体部位；随后将组合后的运动加噪，作为下一步去噪的输入噪声。该迭代过程使各模态能在整体运动语境下不断细化自身贡献，逐步协调跨模态动作，因此生成的运动随迭代愈发自然流畅、协调同步。方法无需对齐的多模态数据。

Result: 在专门构建的多模态基准上进行评估，实验结果表明MOCO优于现有基线，推动了3D Avatar多模态运动生成领域的发展。

Conclusion: MOCO通过解耦式扩散与迭代组装策略，有效解决了并发多模态输入驱动的运动生成中的协调性与数据对齐难题，实现了连贯同步且拟人的Avatar运动生成。

Abstract: It is natural for humans to walk and talk simultaneously. This paper tackles the challenge of replicating such natural behaviors in 3D avatar motion generation driven by concurrent multimodal inputs, such as a text description of a man walking alongside speech audio. Existing methods, constrained by the scarcity of aligned multimodal data, typically combine motions from individual modalities sequentially or through weighted sums. However, they often result in mismatched or unrealistic movements. To overcome these limitations, we propose MOCO, a novel diffusion-based framework capable of processing multiple simultaneous inputs, including speech audio, text descriptions, and trajectory data, to generate coherent and lifelike motions without requiring aligned multimodal data. Our key innovation lies in decoupling the motion generation process. During each denoising step, the diffusion model independently generates motions for each modality from the input noise and assembles the body parts according to predefined spatial rules. The resulting combined motion is then diffused and serves as the input noise for the subsequent denoising step. This iterative approach enables each modality to refine its contribution within the context of the overall motion, progressively harmonizing movements across modalities. Consequently, the generated motions become increasingly natural and fluid with each iteration, achieving coherent and synchronized behaviors. We evaluate our approach using a purpose-built multimodal benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate that MOCO outperforms existing baselines, advancing the field of multimodal motion generation for 3D avatars.

</details>


### [125] [BruNet: A Cross-Domain Transfer Framework for Bruise Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11463)
*Qiming Wang,Richard J. Motley,Ebube E. Obi,Xianfang Sun,Paul L. Rosin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BruNet，将 ViT 视觉编码器（自监督 DINOv3 或预训练 LingBot-Vision）与基于 SAM 的掩码解码器结合，在 HAM10000 上训练、在独立淤伤数据集上零微调评估，声称是首个自动淤伤分割研究，性能优于 CNN、SOTA 分割模型、ChatGPT 辅助 SAM2 零样本基线及 MedSAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淤伤分割在医学影像中面临数据与标注稀缺、边界弥散、外观高度多变等困难。以往相关研究多集中于检测、分类或颜色分析，缺乏像素级定位，本文旨在填补自动淤伤分割这一空白。

Method: 提出 BruNet 分割框架：视觉编码器采用 ViT 结构（自监督 DINOv3 或预训练 LingBot-Vision 骨干），解码器采用基于 SAM 的掩码解码器。模型在 HAM10000 皮肤病变数据集上训练，随后在独立的淤伤数据集上直接评估，不进行额外微调，以考察跨域泛化能力。

Result: BruNet 的表现优于基于 CNN 的模型、当前最先进的分割模型、ChatGPT-4o/5 辅助的 SAM2 零样本基线以及面向医学的 MedSAM，显示出对淤伤分割较强的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: BruNet 通过 ViT 编码器与 SAM 解码器的组合，在零微调跨域设定下实现了优于多种基线的淤伤分割，表明该框架在淤伤分割任务上具有有效性与泛化潜力，并被作者定位为首个自动淤伤分割研究。

Abstract: Segmenting bruises is a challenging task in medical imaging due to limited data and annotations, diffuse boundaries, and highly variable appearance. In this work, we propose BruNet, a segmentation framework that combines a ViT-based visual encoder (a self-supervised DINOv3 or a pretrained LingBot-Vision backbone) with a SAM-based mask decoder. BruNet is trained on the HAM10000 skin lesion dataset and evaluated on a separate bruise dataset without additional fine-tuning. Although a small number of prior studies have explored machine learning and computer vision for bruise analysis, existing work has primarily focused on detection, classification, or colour analysis rather than pixel-level localisation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to address automatic bruise segmentation. Our results show that BruNet outperforms CNN-based models, state-of-the-art segmentation models, ChatGPT-4o/5-assisted SAM2 zero-shot baselines, and the medical-oriented MedSAM model, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalisation to bruise segmentation.

</details>


### [126] [BridgeMatch: Conditional Transport Bridges in Matching Matrix Space for 3D Deformable Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11472)
*Qianliang Wu,Haobo Jiang,Guangwei Gao,Shuo Chen,Jin Xie,Jian Yang,Yaqing Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段生成式求解器，在粗、细分辨率上均保持完整软匹配矩阵，避免top-K剪枝丢失弱正确假设，实现更可靠的刚性与非刚性点云对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的非刚性点云对应关系对可变形解剖配准、具身感知与操作、动态三维重建至关重要。现有由粗到细方法通过选取top-K粗区域来降低计算成本，但这种剪枝会移除弱但正确的假设，并将精细匹配限制在不完整的搜索空间内，从而损害对应质量，尤其在低重叠场景下更为严重。

Method: 提出两阶段生成式求解器，在粗分辨率与高分辨率上均维护完整的软匹配矩阵。阶段I利用去噪扩散在紧凑的粗分辨率空间中估计全局匹配矩阵；随后将该矩阵提升至高分辨率并保持其层级结构，所得提升矩阵具有秩有界与块常量的性质。阶段II通过条件传输桥对其进行精化，桥的实现包含两种动力学形式：确定性端点参数化的条件流匹配（CFM）ODE，以及受Schrödinger桥启发的随机布朗桥SDE。两种变体共享提升后的源分布、时间条件化Transformer以及匹配矩阵端点预测器。

Result: 在4DMatch与4DLoMatch数据集上，两种变体均产生比对比方法更准确的对应关系，并改善下游配准性能，在低重叠情形下增益更大；在CAPE与DeepDeform上，无需目标域自适应、使用同一变形求解器即可提升跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保持粗、细两级的完整软匹配矩阵、并借助生成式传输桥精化，可有效缓解top-K剪枝带来的假设丢失问题，提升非刚性点云对应的准确性与鲁棒性，兼具低重叠场景优势与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Reliable non-rigid point cloud correspondences are important for deformable anatomical registration, embodied perception and manipulation, and dynamic 3D reconstruction. Coarse-to-fine methods reduce computational cost by selecting the top-\(K\) coarse regions. However, this pruning may remove weak but correct hypotheses and restrict fine matching to an incomplete search space. We present \paper, a two-stage generative solver that maintains the complete soft matching matrix at both coarse and high resolutions. Stage~I uses denoising diffusion to estimate a global matching matrix in the compact coarse-resolution space. We then lift this matrix to high resolution while preserving its hierarchy. The lifted matrix is rank-bounded and block-constant. Stage~II refines it through a conditional transport bridge. We implement the bridge with two types of dynamics: a deterministic endpoint-parameterized conditional Flow Matching (CFM) ODE and a stochastic Brownian-bridge SDE inspired by Schrödinger bridges. Both variants share the lifted source, a time-conditioned transformer, and a matching-matrix endpoint predictor. Experiments on 4DMatch and 4DLoMatch show that both variants produce more accurate correspondences than the compared methods and improve downstream registration, with larger gains in low-overlap cases. They also improve cross-dataset generalization on CAPE and DeepDeform without target-domain adaptation while using the same deformation solver.

</details>


### [127] [Pre- and Post-Treatment Brain Metastases Segmentation Using nnU-Net with Post-Processing for BraTS 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11477)
*Haobin Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于5折nnU-Net ResEnc-L集成与规则化后处理流程分割脑转移瘤，验证集LW-DSC为ET 0.733/TC 0.751/WT 0.713/RC 0.549，并通过OOF审计区分了真实提升与仅榜单提升的阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑转移瘤在病灶大小、强化模式和治疗后表现上存在高度病灶间异质性，使治疗前后病例的体积分割成为BraTS 2026 Task 1的核心挑战；同时排行榜增益可能不可靠，需要严格审计。

Method: 采用5折nnU-Net ResEnc-L集成，每折独立训练1000轮，使用标准Dice+交叉熵损失，训练集为1296例四模态病例；随后接规则化后处理级联，针对病灶级Dice（LW-DSC）调优；并用五折OOF分析（无训练泄漏，官方BraTS_evaluation评分）审计每个后处理阶段。

Result: 官方验证排行榜上LW-DSC为ET 0.733、TC 0.751、WT 0.713、RC 0.549；OOF审计确认两个后处理阶段为稳健且逐折一致的提升，第三个阶段仅提升榜单、无法在OOF复现；给出LW-DSC的机理分析，解释召回型后处理风险低而连通域删除风险高；并报告13项负结果。

Conclusion: 规则化后处理需经OOF严格审计才能区分真实泛化增益与榜单过拟合；LW-DSC指标下召回恢复型操作风险可控，而连通域删除需谨慎；代码以Apache-2.0开源。

Abstract: Brain metastases exhibit high inter-lesion variability in size, enhancement pattern, and post-treatment appearance, making volumetric segmentation of both pre- and post-treatment cases the central challenge of the BraTS 2026 Task 1 (Brain Metastases). We build a pragmatic pipeline on a 5-fold nnU-Net ResEnc-L ensemble, in which each fold is trained independently for 1,000 epochs with the standard Dice + cross-entropy loss on 1,296 four-modality training cases. This ensemble is followed by a rule-based post-processing cascade tuned for the lesion-wise Dice similarity coefficient (LW-DSC), a detection-oriented metric that behaves very differently from the traditional global Dice. The final pipeline reaches an LW-DSC of 0.733 / 0.751 / 0.713 / 0.549 on the enhancing tumour (ET), tumour core (TC), whole tumour (WT), and resection cavity (RC) sub-regions on the official validation leaderboard. Rather than trusting these leaderboard gains, we audit every post-processing stage with a five-fold out-of-fold (OOF) analysis with no model-training leakage over all 1,296 training cases, scored with the official BraTS evaluation code (BraTS_evaluation): it confirms two stages as robust, per-fold-consistent improvements while the third improves only the leaderboard and does not reproduce out-of-fold. We further provide a mechanistic analysis of the LW-DSC metric that explains why recall-recovering post-processing carries low risk whereas component deletion does not, and we report thirteen negative results spanning loss engineering, alternative backbones, and inference-time settings, several of which run counter to widely held intuitions. Source code is released under Apache-2.0 at https://github.com/hornbeamliu/brats2026-met.

</details>


### [128] [FreeFlow: A Bias-free Hierarchical Transformer for Optical Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11486)
*Vladislav Bargatin,Alexander Yakovenko,Khaled Abud,Dmitriy Vatolin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeFlow 是一种不依赖光流专用归纳偏置的层次化 Transformer，通过组合三种注意力机制构建单一前馈编解码器，在主要基准上达到 SOTA，并在 1080p 推理中保持内存高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光流方法依赖任务特定的归纳偏置（如相关体积、特征扭曲、迭代细化等），虽有效但将模型限制在预设启发式内，降低表达力，并导致流程复杂与额外计算开销。作者希望探索无需光流专用组件、结构更简洁的替代方案。

Method: 提出 FreeFlow：一种层次化 Transformer，不含任何光流专用组件，仅采用单一前馈编码器-解码器结构。该架构组合三种注意力变体：窗口注意力用于局部处理、移位窗口注意力用于跨窗口信息交换、以及低分辨率下运行的全局注意力。该设计可随模型容量自然扩展。

Result: 尽管缺少标准归纳偏置，FreeFlow 在主要基准上取得 SOTA：Sintel Clean/Final 上 EPE 为 0.68/1.48，KITTI-2015 上 Fl-all 为 3.23，Spring 上 1px 为 3.192；同时从较小到较大模型变体可带来一致的精度提升，并在 1080p 推理时保持内存高效。

Conclusion: FreeFlow 表明，不依赖光流特定归纳偏置的纯 Transformer 架构同样可以实现高精度光流估计，且具备良好的可扩展性与内存效率，为简化光流流水线提供了新思路。

Abstract: Optical flow methods typically rely on task-specific inductive biases, such as correlation volumes, feature warping, and iterative refinement, among others, to reach high accuracy. While effective, such biases constrain the model to predefined heuristics, which can limit its expressivity and lead to more complex pipelines and additional computational cost. We present FreeFlow, a hierarchical transformer built without any flow-specific components, using instead a single feed-forward encoder--decoder. FreeFlow combines three attention variants: window attention for local processing, shifted-window attention for cross-window information exchange, and a global attention operating at a reduced resolution. The resulting architecture scales naturally with model capacity, enabling a consistent accuracy gain from small to large variants. Despite the absence of standard inductive biases, FreeFlow achieves state-of-the-art results on major benchmarks, including Sintel (0.68/1.48 EPE on Clean/Final), KITTI-2015 (3.23 Fl-all), and Spring (3.192 1px), while remaining memory efficient at 1080p inference.

</details>


### [129] [Recursive Code World Models: Building Complex Worlds through Recursive Scene Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11499)
*Zhiqi Li,Yuxuan Liao,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出递归代码世界模型（RCWM），通过递归场景程序表示与递归调用自身的构建求解器，从单张参考图像重建复杂三维世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码世界模型虽能将世界表示为可执行程序，但该表示本身无法决定如何构建复杂世界，因此需要一套系统化的构建方法。

Method: RCWM 将递归场景程序（RSP）表示与递归求解器耦合：每次求解调用都遵循"建立整体—递归重建未解决部分—回访整体以精修组合"的全局-局部-全局流程；参考对齐视图在层级间传播共享相机投影；父级回访处理局部精修后出现的边界、空间关系与共享误差；视觉-语言编码智能体通过比较参考图像与场景渲染来指导精修、递归下降与返回。

Result: 在复杂场景中，RCWM 优于先前的基于代码的图像到场景重建方法；消融实验支持递归构建的收益，并表明更深的递归调用可改善更细尺度的重建。

Conclusion: RCWM 为从视觉证据构建复杂可执行世界提供了一种递归构建原理。

Abstract: Code world models represent worlds as executable programs, but this representation alone does not determine how to construct a complex world. We introduce Recursive Code World Models (RCWM), a framework for reconstructing complex 3D worlds in code from a single reference image. RCWM couples a Recursive Scene Program (RSP) representation with a construction solver that recursively calls itself. An RSP represents the executable world as compositional scene code, while each solver call follows the same complete process: establish the whole, recursively reconstruct unresolved parts, and revisit the whole to refine their composition. This global-local-global recursion gives fine-scale structures their own perception-and-editing loops while preserving scene-wide geometry and relationships. Reference-aligned views propagate a shared camera projection across levels, while parent revisitation addresses boundaries, spatial relations, and shared errors that emerge after local refinement. A vision-language coding agent directly compares reference images with scene renders to guide refinement, recursive descent, and return. Across complex scenes, RCWM outperforms prior code-based image-to-scene reconstruction methods. Ablation studies further support the benefits of recursive construction and suggest that deeper calls can improve finer-scale reconstruction. RCWM provides a recursive construction principle for building complex executable worlds from visual evidence.

</details>


### [130] [UBone3D: Physics-Rectified Conditional Flow Matching for Anatomical 3D Shape Completion from Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11506)
*Weiying Chen,Yuchong Gao,Siyuan Li,Marek Reformat,Rui Zheng,Edmond Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UBone3D 提出基于物理校正条件流匹配（CFM）的三维超声点云补全框架，利用 CT 训练的形状先验 BoneFM 与超声成像空间物理一致性网络 USimNet 双重引导，在模拟与活体数据上显著提升重建精度与解剖保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维超声安全、无辐射，适合纵向监测，但分割得到的部分点云伪影严重（表面增厚、条纹、缺失等），从这类点云恢复干净完整的解剖结构十分困难；现有方法未建模超声成像的确定性物理伪影，导致补全结果解剖不合理或与物理观测不一致。

Method: 提出 UBone3D：基于物理校正的条件流匹配（CFM）直接由部分超声观测补全点云。1）用模拟的物理代理（physics proxy）建模确定性伪影（表面增厚、条纹、缺失）；2）引入测试时物理校正（test-time physics rectification）引导形状补全；3）推理时由两个解耦力共同驱动：CT 训练的生成形状先验 BoneFM 保证解剖合理性，USimNet 在超声成像空间施加物理一致性。

Result: 在模拟数据和活体（in-vivo）数据上，相比现有基线方法，重建精度与解剖保真度均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 将确定性超声物理伪影显式建模并注入生成式补全流程，可在无辐射三维超声下实现解剖合理且物理一致的骨骼点云补全，验证了物理校正 CFM 与解耦双先验引导的有效性。

Abstract: Three-dimensional ultrasound (US) is a safe, radiation-free complementary modality to CT and X-rays for longitudinal monitoring, yet its segmentation-derived partial point clouds are extremely artifact-laden. Consequently, it is challenging to recover a clean and complete anatomical structure from such US point clouds. In this paper, we present UBone3D, a novel framework based on physics-rectified conditional flow matching (CFM) that performs point cloud completion directly from partial US observations. UBone3D models deterministic physics artifacts (e.g., surface thickening, streaking, dropouts) via a simulated physics proxy, and introduces test-time physics rectification to steer the shape completion. At inference, the completion is jointly steered by two decoupled forces: (1) anatomical plausibility enforced by a CT-trained generative shape prior, BoneFM, and (2) physics consistency enforced by USimNet in the ultrasound formation space. Extensive experiments on simulated and in-vivo data demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy and anatomical fidelity over existing baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Harnessing Intrinsic Subject-Aware Attention for Controllable Multi-Subject Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11507)
*Niange Yu,Ye Tian,Biaolong Chen,Miao Lu,Aixi Zhang,Hao Jiang,Yunhai Tong,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过分析DiT内部注意力机制，发现其天然形成"内在空间定位图(ISGM)"，据此提出双阶段内在注意力利用框架DIAL，实现可控保真度与缓解语义漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多主体视频生成面临两大挑战：保真强度不可控，以及潜在的语义漂移。论文希望利用模型内部信号从根本上解决这两个问题，而非依赖外部模块或重新训练。

Method: 首先分析Diffusion Transformers的内部机制，发现某些注意力块天然形成可用于精确定位参考主体的Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map (ISGM)。基于此提出DIAL框架：在低噪声阶段，用ISGM引导注意力机制，在推理时无需重训练即可精确控制保真强度；在高噪声阶段，用同样的图自动构建偏好对，以零额外成本进行强化学习，将模型注意力锚定到参考主体。

Result: 在OpenS2V-Eval基准上，DIAL显著优于基线模型，持续提升身份一致性，并实现可控的保真强度。

Conclusion: DIAL证明了DiT内部注意力信号可被有效复用，同时服务于推理时的可控保真与训练时的抗漂移，为多主体视频生成提供了一种高效、无需额外标注的统一方案。

Abstract: Multi-subject video generation faces two key challenges: uncontrollable fidelity strength and potential semantic drift. We address these by analyzing the internal mechanisms of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs). We found that certain attention blocks naturally form an Intrinsic Spatial Grounding Map (ISGM) that precisely locates reference subjects. Building on this insight, we propose Dual-phase Intrinsic Attention Leveraging (DIAL), a framework that uses these internal signals for both training and inference. In low-noise stages, we use ISGM to guide the attention mechanism, allowing precise control over fidelity strength during inference without retraining. In high-noise stages, we use these same maps to automatically build preference pairs at no additional cost for Reinforcement Learning (RL). This RL procedure effectively anchors the model's attention to reference subjects and mitigates semantic drift. Extensive experiments show that DIAL significantly outperforms baseline models on the OpenS2V-Eval benchmark, consistently improving identity consistency and enabling controllable fidelity strength.

</details>


### [132] [Prototype Matters: Modality-unified Prototype Self-distillation for Unsupervised Visible-infrared Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11514)
*Menglin Wang,Xiaojin Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出模态统一原型对比与原型引导自蒸馏的统一框架，用于无监督可见光-红外行人重识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督可见光-红外行人重识别中，跨模态关联估计至关重要。现有最优传输方法存在硬标签分配过于刚性、忽略聚类噪声的问题；且仅施加跨模态对比也是次优的，无法同时优化模态内与模态间的相似性关系。

Method: 利用原型进行跨模态学习：一是不与跨模态原型对比，而是采用模态统一原型对比，联合且同时优化模态内与跨模态的相似性关系以增强模态不变性；二是以自原型作为稳定教师，通过原型引导自蒸馏在线精炼实例-原型关系；两部分在统一框架中优化。

Result: 在标准VI-ReID基准上进行了充分的对比与分析，验证了所提方法的有效性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过模态统一原型对比与原型引导自蒸馏的统一优化，构建了简单而有效的模型，提升了无监督可见光-红外行人重识别性能。

Abstract: Estimating reliable cross-modality association is crucial to unsupervised visible-infrared person re-ID. While optimal transport is shown to be a practical solution for cross-modality association, it suffers from the rigidness of hard label assignment without considering the impact of cluster noise. Moreover, enforcing only cross-modality contrast is also suboptimal, as it fails to jointly optimize the similarity relation within and across modality. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for cross-modality learning by well exploitation of prototypes: First, instead of contrasting with cross-modality prototypes, we show that modality-unified prototypical contrast facilitates better modality invariance by jointly and simultaneously optimizing similarity relation within and across-modality. Taking self-prototype as a steady teacher, we further refine the instance-prototype online relation through prototype-guided self-distillation. The two components are optimized in a unified framework, leading to a simple yet effective model. On standard VI-ReID benchmarks, we perform extensive comparison and analysis, validating the effectiveness of our proposed method. Code is available at: https://github.com/Terminator8758/PoSeD.

</details>


### [133] [LoopVAE: Recurrent Depth Across Scales for Visual Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11516)
*Zhiying Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopVAE 提出在视觉分词器中跨尺度复用同一套尺度与循环条件化核心参数，仅保留分辨率转换模块独立，从而以更少参数实现高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层视觉分词器通常为不同空间尺度分配不同的处理模块，参数量较大。作者希望探究其中有多少计算可以共享同一套参数，以降低模型规模。

Method: 采用尺度与循环条件化的核心模块，在同一尺度内及跨尺度间复用；分辨率变化的转换层保持独立。四块核心在编码器或解码器中执行 28 次块应用。使用卷积与 Transformer 算子，并支持单分辨率或多分辨率潜在接口。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，29M 参数卷积模型达到 0.28 rFID 与 32.54 dB PSNR，相比 84M 参考 VAE 减少约 65% 参数。非对抗 Transformer 消融显示全局共享下 PSNR/SSIM 有竞争力，非共享块改善 LPIPS。循环干预表明完成训练递归可提升重建，小特征更新也会产生显著下游影响；截断会暴露输出范围错误。运行时分析显示参数更少但算术更多、耗时更长。

Conclusion: LoopVAE 证明跨尺度的循环深度是一种有效的参数共享设计轴，可用于视觉分词，并在卷积与 Transformer 算子、单/多分辨率潜在接口下均适用。

Abstract: Hierarchical visual tokenizers typically allocate different processing blocks to different spatial scales. We ask how much of this computation can use the same parameters. LoopVAE reuses a scale- and loop-conditioned core within and across scales, while keeping resolution-changing transitions independent. A four-block core executes 28 block applications per encoder or decoder. On ImageNet-256, the 29M-parameter convolutional model reaches 0.28 rFID and 32.54 dB PSNR under an approximately 30-epoch two-stage training budget, using approximately 65% fewer parameters than the 84M reference VAEs. A non-adversarial Transformer ablation with the same execution graph finds competitive PSNR and SSIM under global sharing, although unshared blocks improve LPIPS. Targeted loop interventions show that completing the trained recurrence improves reconstruction and that even small feature updates can have substantial downstream effects. Truncation also exposes output-range errors, distinguishing useful recurrent computation from reliable early exit.
