Runtime profiling reveals the execution tradeoff: fewer stored weights require more arithmetic and longer runtime in the tested configurations. With convolutional and Transformer operators and single- or multi-resolution latent interfaces, LoopVAE establishes recurrent depth across scales as a parameter-sharing design axis for visual tokenization.

</details>


### [134] [Learning Interaction between Image and Layout Priors for Joint Image-Layout Generation in Design Templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11519)
*Shirong Yang,Bo Yang,Ying Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InterIL模型，通过连接预训练图像扩散模型与布局扩散模型的主干并引入可学习通信模块，实现背景图像与前景布局的联合生成，双向建模图像-布局交互，在图像、布局及协调性上优于既有顺序式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图形设计模板创建需由输入文本生成背景图像及其上的前景元素布局，形成协调构图。既有工作多采用顺序生成范式，依次生成各设计元素，难以忠实刻画背景与布局之间的依赖关系（即联合图像-布局分布），从而限制生成模板质量。

Method: 提出InterIL：在单一生成过程中联合生成背景图像与布局两种模态。用可学习通信模块连接预训练图像扩散模型与布局扩散模型的主干，显式建模双向图像-布局交互。训练时冻结图像与布局主干以保留并利用大规模预训练单模态先验，仅更新通信模块，使模型专注学习跨模态交互。模型不含设计特化的归纳偏置，以保留真实设计原有特征；并引入测试时引导策略，供用户在推理阶段施加偏好而无需重训练。

Result: 实验表明，相较此前方法，本模型在图像质量、布局质量及图像-布局协调性上均显著更优，生成结果更接近真实样本；并验证了在推理阶段无需重训练即可灵活施加用户偏好。

Conclusion: 联合生成范式优于顺序生成范式，能更好捕获图像-布局联合分布并提升构图协调性；冻结预训练主干、仅训练通信模块是高效利用单模态先验的可行方案，且天然支持测试时可控性。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the problem of graphic design template creation, which generates a background image and a layout of foreground elements over the background to form a harmonious composition from an input text.
  Prior work on graphic design generation mostly adopts a sequential paradigm, where design elements are generated sequentially. We argue that such a sequential scheme falls short of faithfully capturing the dependency between the background and layout (and thus the joint image-layout distribution), which limits the quality of generated design templates.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose a model, InterIL, which jointly generates the two modalities, background image and layout, in a single generative process. The novel design of our joint model connects the backbones of pretrained image and layout diffusion models with a learnable communication module to explicitly model bidirectional image-layout interaction. During training, the image and layout backbones are frozen to maintain and leverage the vast pretrained single-modality prior knowledge, while only the communication module is updated, so that the model can focus on learning image-layout interaction and thereby better capture the joint image-layout distribution for improved composition harmony.
  Our model has no design-specific inductive bias, which allows it to better preserve the original characteristics of realistic designs. We further introduce a test-time guidance strategy to enable users to impose their specific preferences on generated results.
  Our experiments show that, compared with prior approaches, our model can generate significantly better results in terms of image, layout and image-layout harmonization, producing outputs closer to real samples. We also demonstrate the flexibility of our model in enforcing user preferences at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [135] [World in World: Explore the World with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11548)
*Chenxi Song,Yanming Yang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World in World，一个免训练的推理时接口，将异构控制证据统一为相机与时间标注的干净视觉状态，通过冻结因果视频模型的原生自注意力读取，实现相机控制重渲染、长时程重访与人体动作迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频世界模型支持交互式长时程探索，但灵活控制困难：从新视角探索源视频要求生成结果与记录事件同步、将观测内容置于指定视角、合理补全新暴露区域、并在重访时恢复已生成外观。现有方法依赖任务专用模块或额外训练。

Method: 训练自由的推理时接口。证据包括：源视频观测、目标视角场景投影、引导新暴露主体区域补全的几何渲染、以及滚动缓存之外检索到的已生成状态。每类证据带token级支持与各自可用性调度。对应路由器结合持久点身份与几何建立token对应，将受支持查询导向匹配的源视频token；证据级注意力CFG（EWA）利用同一去噪前向传播中的注意力响应，独立调节各辅助通道的额外贡献。

Result: 在多样视角变化下的相机控制视频重渲染任务上评估，从感知质量、时间一致性与相机跟随精度三方面进行测量。

Conclusion: 同一冻结骨干网络下的共享接口可支持相机控制重渲染、长时程重访与人体动作迁移，无需针对任务微调。

Abstract: Autoregressive video world models enable interactive, long-horizon exploration, but flexible control remains challenging. Exploring a source video from new viewpoints requires the generated rollout to remain synchronised with the recorded event, place observed content in the requested view, plausibly complete newly exposed regions, and recover previously generated appearance on revisits. Existing methods typically address these requirements through task-specific modules or additional training. We present World in World, a training-free inference-time interface that converts heterogeneous control evidence into camera- and time-labelled clean visual states, which are read through the native self attention of a frozen causal video model. The evidence comprises source-video observations, target-view scene projections, geometry renderings that guide completion of newly exposed subject regions, and retrieved generated states beyond the rolling cache. Each evidence source carries token-level support and its own availability schedule. A correspondence router combines persistent point identities with geometry to establish token correspondences, guiding supported queries towards matching source-video tokens. Evidence-wise attention CFG (EWA) then independently regulates each auxiliary channel's additional contribution using attention responses from the same denoising forward pass. The shared interface supports camera-controlled rerendering, long-horizon revisiting, and human-motion transfer with the same frozen backbone. We evaluate World in World on camera-controlled video rerendering under diverse viewpoint changes, assessing perceptual quality, temporal consistency, and camera-following accuracy.

</details>


### [136] [A Comparative Evaluation of Pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks for Melanoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11550)
*Wagner Moreno Schmitz,Marco Antonio de Castro Barbosa,Thiago Magalhães Amaral,Dalcimar Casanova,Jefferson Tales Oliva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在相同训练协议下，评估了ResNet50、VGG16、VGG19、MobileNet和InceptionV3五种预训练CNN在皮肤病变分类中的表现，覆盖皮肤镜与组织病理学两类图像模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑色素瘤早期诊断对提高患者生存率至关重要，但病变类型间视觉高度相似且图像采集条件多变，准确区分黑色素瘤与其他皮肤病变仍是临床难题。已有大量预训练CNN架构，但针对特定成像模态如何选择最合适的架构仍是未解问题。

Method: 在HAM10000、ISIC 2018和CR-AI4SkIN数据集上，采用统一训练协议，对ResNet50、VGG16、VGG19、MobileNet和InceptionV3五种预训练CNN进行皮肤病变分类评估，并比较其在皮肤镜与组织病理学图像上的表现。

Result: 皮肤镜图像上准确率为71%（InceptionV3，ISIC 2018）至84%（ResNet50，HAM10000）；组织病理学图像（CR-AI4SkIN）上准确率为72%（VGG19）至83%（ResNet50）。

Conclusion: 模型性能在皮肤镜与组织病理学图像模态间存在差异：在皮肤镜图像上表现相近的架构，在组织病理学数据上表现不同，说明架构选择需结合具体成像模态。

Abstract: Early diagnosis of melanoma is critical for improving patient survival rates. However, accurately distinguishing melanoma from other skin lesions remains a significant clinical challenge due to the high visual similarity among lesion types and variability in image acquisition conditions. Artificial intelligence, particularly machine learning, has emerged as a promising tool to support dermatological diagnosis by automating feature extraction from medical images. Among the available approaches, convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have demonstrated strong performance in image classification tasks, making them well-suited for analyzing both dermatoscopic and histopathological images, given their ability to capture hierarchical visual patterns relevant to lesion characterization. Nevertheless, despite numerous pre-trained CNN architectures having been proposed, selecting the most appropriate one for a given imaging modality remains an open challenge. In this study, we evaluate pre-trained convolutional neural networks (CNNs) for skin lesion classification using dermatoscopic and histopathological image datasets. Experiments were conducted on the HAM10000, ISIC 2018, and CR-AI4SkIN datasets, evaluating the ResNet50, VGG16, VGG19, MobileNet, and InceptionV3 architectures under the same training protocol. The experimental evaluation showed that the models achieved accuracies ranging from 71% (InceptionV3 on ISIC 2018) to 84% (ResNet50 on HAM10000) on dermatoscopic images. For histopathological images, accuracies ranged from 72% (VGG19) to 83% (ResNet50) on the CR-AI4SkIN dataset. The results demonstrate that model performance differs between dermatoscopic and histopathological image modalities, showing that architectures exhibiting similar performance on dermatoscopic images exhibit different performance on histopathological data.

</details>


### [137] [Learn the Solid, Not the File: Canonical Inputs for Neural Networks on CAD Boundary Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11573)
*Heinrich Jiang,Hager Yasser Mohamed,Alexander Hitt,Valeriia Lomakina,Henning Jiang,Jennifer Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出同一实体可对应多种B-rep表示，导致现有B-rep编码器对表示变化不鲁棒；作者提出基于实体自身的规范区域图（canonical region graph）作为输入表示，理论上保证重分区与刚体运动的等变性，在标准基准上匹配最强基线且对所有扰动稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-rep是CAD参数化3D模型的标准格式，但同一实体可被不同操作、几何内核重建、导出设置等因素表示成不同的B-rep。现有B-rep编码器在这种"同实体、异表示"的扰动下表现脆弱，甚至在标准基准上出现性能崩塌，因此需要一种对表示变化鲁棒的输入表示。

Method: 作者构建了FreeCAD人工数据集以度量设计者建模同一零件时的表示差异，并系统性评估现有B-rep编码器在标准基准扰动、CAD软件固有差异及人工建模差异下的鲁棒性。为解决该问题，提出"规范区域图"（canonical region graph）：其节点、特征与坐标系均从实体本身导出，并给出在面重分区与刚体运动下的理论不变性保证。

Result: 现有流行B-rep编码器在多种扰动下性能大幅下降甚至崩溃；所提出的规范区域图在标准基准上与最强基线持平，同时在所有测试扰动下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 现有B-rep编码范式对同一实体的不同表示不鲁棒，规范区域图通过从实体本身导出表示并具备重分区与刚体运动的等变性保证，为鲁棒的B-rep学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Boundary representation (B-rep) is the standard format used by modern CAD systems for parametric 3D models. It turns out, the exact same solid can be represented by different B-reps: for example, two engineers using different operations, a geometry kernel rebuilding the file, and an export setting repartitioning faces will lead to different B-reps even though the underlying solid remains the same.
  We show that existing B-rep encoders are not robust to variation in the B-rep with the same solid on perturbations applied to standard benchmarks, naturally occurring variations inherent to CAD software, and differences in how designers model the same part via a human dataset we created in FreeCAD. The performance of popular B-rep encoders often collapses catastrophically.
  We propose the canonical region graph, an input representation whose nodes, features and coordinate frame are derived from the solid itself and show theoretical invariance guarantees on repartitioning and rigid motions. It matches the strongest baseline on standard benchmarks, and is stable under every perturbation we test.

</details>


### [138] [OmniKVQuant: KV Cache Quantization for Omni-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11582)
*Suho Yoo,Hyunjong Ok,Jongmin Choi,Jihoo Jung,Joon Son Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的 OmniKVQuant 框架，针对多模态 KV 缓存量化中的时序键漂移与异构值几何问题，实现 2-bit KV 缓存并保持多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni-LLM 同时处理音频、视频与文本，KV 缓存显存开销显著增长；文本 LLM 中常用的 KV 缓存量化方法在多模态场景下尚未被探索。

Method: 分析代表性旋转式 KV 量化方法 TurboQuant 在多模态缓存上的行为，发现时序键漂移与异构值几何两个问题；提出 OmniKVQuant：(i) 在输入流的每个短窗口上设置键量化范围；(ii) 按模态分别对值进行旋转。并实现融合 Triton 解码核，在注意力计算中解包 2-bit 缓存。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Omni 与 Qwen3-Omni 上，OmniKVQuant 支持 2-bit KV 缓存，并能在七个音视频基准上大幅保持性能；融合 Triton 解码核避免构建稠密 FP16 缓存。

Conclusion: OmniKVQuant 为 Omni-LLM 提供免训练、高效的 KV 缓存量化方案，兼顾显存压缩与多模态性能。

Abstract: As Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) take in audio, video and text together, their KV cache memory cost grows. KV cache quantization is the de facto approach in text-only LLMs, but its application to Omni-LLMs remains unexplored. In this paper, we analyze how TurboQuant, a representative rotation-based KV cache quantization method, behaves on multimodal caches and identify two critical issues: temporal key drift and heterogeneous value geometry. To address these, we propose OmniKVQuant, a training-free framework that (i) sets the key quantization range over each short window of the input stream; and (ii) rotates values separately per modality. On Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni, OmniKVQuant enables 2-bit KV caches while substantially preserving performance across seven audio-visual benchmarks. We further provide a fused Triton decode kernel that unpacks the 2-bit cache during attention, so no dense FP16 cache is ever built. Code: https://github.com/kaistmm/OmniKVQuant

</details>


### [139] [MMGait: Benchmarking and Unifying Gait Recognition across Heterogeneous Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11601)
*Saihui Hou,Chenye Wang,Qingyuan Cai,Aoqi Li,Yongzhen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多传感器步态基准 MMGait 与统一识别框架 OmniGait++，将单模态、跨模态与多模态步态识别纳入共享身份空间，实现对不同模态子集的统一处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态识别多基于 RGB 视频或其衍生的轮廓、姿态，而人体行走同时产生光度、几何与运动等异构线索，RGB 中心化基准难以系统考察这些线索，且单模态、模态对与融合配置通常需分别训练专家，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: 构建 MMGait 多传感器基准，将可见光、红外、深度、LiDAR 与雷达观测在序列级对齐，覆盖外观、轮廓、几何、运动与体结构等模态；在共享的冒充者增强协议下评测单模态、跨模态有向检索与多模态识别。进而提出 Omni-Modal Gait Recognition 与 OmniGait++：采用模态专属前端加共享身份编码器，保留模态相关线索并学习可比身份描述；以锚点引导融合模块聚合不同规模模态子集，无需帧级同步，单一联合训练权重覆盖三类设置。

Result: 模态排序随探测条件变化，跨模态对齐仍然困难，融合常带来互补增益；OmniGait++ 在多个共享设置下与任务专属专家相当，并可扩展至固定模态对模型无法支持的高基数融合。

Conclusion: MMGait 可作为异构步态感知的通用测试平台，验证了在模态可用性变化下统一步态识别的可行性。

Abstract: Gait recognition is commonly studied using RGB videos or their derived silhouettes and poses. Yet human walking produces heterogeneous photometric, geometric, and motion cues that cannot be systematically examined with RGB-centered benchmarks. We present MMGait, a large-scale multi-sensor benchmark that brings visible, infrared, depth, LiDAR, and radar observations into sequence-level correspondence. It provides diverse modalities spanning appearance, contours, geometry, motion, and body structure. Under a shared impostor-augmented protocol, we evaluate single-modal recognition, cross-modal recognition via directed retrieval, and multi-modal recognition using task-specific experts. Across settings, modality rankings vary with probe conditions, cross-modal alignment remains difficult, and fusion often provides complementary gains. This analysis exposes a scalability problem: individual modalities, modality pairs, and fusion configurations are typically handled by separately trained experts. We formulate Omni-Modal Gait Recognition, which unifies single-modal, cross-modal, and multi-modal recognition within a shared identity space. OmniGait++ uses modality-specific front ends followed by a shared identity encoder to preserve modality-dependent cues while learning comparable identity descriptors. An anchor-guided fusion module aggregates modality subsets of varying size without frame-level synchronization. A jointly trained checkpoint covers all three recognition settings and accommodates modality subsets of different compositions and cardinalities. Experiments show OmniGait++ remains competitive with task-specific experts in many shared settings and extends to higher-cardinality fusion unavailable to fixed-pair models. The results establish MMGait as a common testbed for heterogeneous gait sensing and demonstrate the feasibility of unified recognition under varying modality availability.

</details>


### [140] [LangStreet: Persistent Language Fields for Anchor-Decoded Street Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11616)
*Runyi Yang,Deheng Zhang,Xiaoye Wang,Mengjiao Ma,Lei Sun,Kanzhi Wu,Ajad Chhatkuli,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"语义归属"思想构建面向视图条件高斯场景的持久化语言场，仅存储锚点特征与紧凑槽残差即可在近零精度损失下大幅压缩存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言高斯场通常假设承载语义的基元跨视图可识别，但在可扩展的锚点解码表示中，持久锚点生成随相机变化的视图条件子高斯，其几何与外观随视角改变，导致该假设失效。

Method: 核心为语义归属：瞬态子高斯负责路由观测，持久解码槽及其父锚点拥有语言场；利用alpha合成责任在槽上累积加性方向证据，这些统计量可精确边缘化到锚点；对弱支持槽用锚点对齐证据补全并保持锚点方向；用锚点相对语义坐标下的低秩残差表示槽细节。给出三个变体：base存锚点特征加紧凑槽残差，light仅存锚点特征，max显式存完整维度补全槽特征。

Result: 无需场景特定语义优化，base在KITTI、Virtual KITTI、Waymo上接近max。KITTI上base达34.19 2D mIoU、有效特征占用2.72 GiB，max为34.20 mIoU、12.90 GiB；精度-存储趋势在另两个数据集上一致。

Conclusion: 视图条件splat上的语言场需要持久语义归属、守恒证据以及平衡稳定性、细节与表示成本的层级结构；代码、检查点与基准套件将公开。

Abstract: Language Gaussian fields implicitly assume that the primitive carrying semantics remains identifiable across views. This assumption breaks in scalable anchor-decoded representations, where persistent anchors generate view-conditioned child Gaussians whose geometry and appearance vary with the camera. We introduce Ours, a persistent language field for such structured Gaussian scenes. Our key idea is semantic ownership: transient children route observations, while persistent decoder slots and their parent anchors own the language field. We use alpha-compositing responsibilities to accumulate additive directional evidence at slots; these statistics marginalize exactly to anchors. We then complete weakly supported slots with anchor-aligned evidence while preserving the anchor direction, and represent slot detail through low-rank residuals in anchor-relative semantic coordinates. Our primary model, Ours (base), stores anchor features together with compact slot residuals. Ours (light) retains only anchor features, whereas Ours (max) stores the full-dimensional completed slot features explicitly. Without scene-specific semantic optimization, Ours (base) nearly matches Ours (max) across KITTI, Virtual KITTI, and Waymo. On KITTI, it achieves 34.19 2D mIoU with a 2.72 GiB effective feature footprint, compared with 34.20 mIoU and 12.90 GiB for Ours (max). The same accuracy-storage trend holds on Virtual KITTI and Waymo. These results show that language fields on view-conditioned splats require persistent semantic ownership, conserved evidence, and a hierarchy that balances stability, detail, and representation cost. Our code, checkpoints, and benchmark suite will be publicly available.

</details>


### [141] [Vidu S2: Real-Time Interactive, Editable, and Spatial Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11638)
*Jintao Zhang,Kai Jiang,Jintao Chen,Xu Wang,Deyuan Liu,Jungang Li,Dechuang Chen,Ming Lin,Jingjiang Zhou,Haopeng Jin,Qi Jia,Xiaohang Wang,Yaole Wang,Zhanqiang Zhang,Ran Li,Zhengkun Huang,Shuyue Xiong,Yuji Wang,Zikun Dai,Hui He,Yang Luo,Mang Ning,Weiqi Feng,Chengyang Ye,Xinyue Lin,Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Hengkai Tan,Zeyuan Wang,Chendong Xiang,Kaiwen Zheng,Zhijie Deng,Fan Bao,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Vidu S2 系统，包含实时交互数字人模型 Vidu S2-Avatar 与实时视频编辑模型 Vidu S2-Editing，并探索了二者的实时空间视频生成可行性，性能超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Vidu S1 基础上，进一步提升实时视频生成的清晰度、动态参考能力与指令跟随能力，并将实时编辑与数字人交互整合到统一系统，同时探索空间视频生成的可行性。

Method: 提出 Vidu S2 系统，由 Vidu S2-Avatar（实时交互数字人）与 Vidu S2-Editing（实时视频编辑）两个模型组成，并在此基础上探索实时空间视频生成方案。

Result: Vidu S2-Avatar 支持实时 720p 视频生成、可随时更新的动态参考以及更强的指令跟随（如舞蹈动作）；Vidu S2-Editing 支持对视频流进行实时编辑，涵盖风格渲染、服装替换、角色替换与背景替换。实验表明 Vidu S2 优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: Vidu S2 实现了实时交互数字人与实时视频编辑的一体化，并验证了实时空间视频生成的可行性，相关在线演示已公开。

Abstract: We present Vidu S2, which comprises Vidu S2-Avatar, a real-time interactive digital-character model, and Vidu S2-Editing, a real-time video editing model. Moreover, we explore the feasibility of real-time spatial video generation for both Vidu S2-Avatar and Vidu S2-Editing. Compared with Vidu S1, Vidu S2-Avatar supports real-time 720p video generation, generation with dynamic references that can be updated at any moment, and stronger instruction following, such as dancing. Vidu S2-Editing supports editing a video stream in real time, including style rendering, clothing replacement, character replacement, and background replacement. Experiments show that Vidu S2 outperforms all baselines. A playable online demo is available at https://vidu.com/vidu-stream.

</details>


### [142] [Self-Supervised Cardiac Phase Detection via Single-Parameter Latent Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11650)
*John Bonnici,Matthew Baugh,Aleksandra Kulbaka,Sarah Cechnicka,Bernhard Kainz,Alberto Gomez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将心脏相位约束为潜在空间单参数轨道的心电相位自监督建模方法，实现ED/ES的准确识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图中ED/ES帧的手动选择主观性强，存在明显的操作者间差异。现有自监督方法要么强制严格周期轨迹（可解释但假设过强），要么学习无约束低维运动子空间（相位隐式，需后处理几何操作恢复ED/ES）。作者希望借助生理先验实现既可解释又灵活的相位表示。

Method: 将“心脏相位是一维信号”的生理观察转化为先验：把潜在运动分量约束为单参数潜在轨道，即潜在空间中的全局线性轨迹，由有界标量相位变量索引；再通过正弦非线性映射该变量，得到具有一致时间顺序的振荡运动信号，从而可直接从学习到的相位信号中识别ED与ES。该方法保持捕捉不规则心跳的灵活性，仅在EchoNet-Dynamic上无标注训练。

Result: 该极简单参数心脏相位模型学习到有效的潜在轨道，在ED定位上显著优于此前最优方法，在ES定位上与之相当，且使用了更受约束的表示和更少的训练轮次。

Conclusion: 原则性的生理归纳偏置能够匹配甚至超越更复杂表示的性能，说明将心脏相位的一维本质作为先验可有效提升ED/ES识别的可解释性与准确性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate identification of end-diastole (ED) and end-systole (ES) in echocardiography underpins the quantification of ventricular function, yet manual selection of these key frames is subjective and introduces clinically significant inter-operator variability. Recent self-supervised methods either prescribe strict periodic trajectories or learn an unconstrained low-dimensional motion subspace from reconstruction or registration objectives. The former offers interpretability but imposes restrictive assumptions on temporal progression, whereas the latter leaves cardiac phase implicit and ED/ES must be recovered through post-hoc geometric processing of the learned trajectory. We translate the physiological observation that cardiac phase is a one-dimensional signal into a prior by constraining the latent motion component to a single-parameter latent orbit, i.e., a global linear trajectory in latent space indexed by a bounded scalar phase variable. Mapping this variable through a sinusoidal nonlinearity yields an oscillatory motion signal with consistent temporal ordering, enabling direct identification of ED and ES from the learned phase signal. This inductive bias allows the model to capture an interpretable representation of the cardiac cycle, while maintaining flexibility to capture irregular heartbeats. Trained on EchoNet-Dynamic without annotations, our minimal single-parameter cardiac phase model learns an effective latent orbit, significantly improves upon the previous state of the art in ED localisation and matches it in ES localisation while using a more constrained representation and fewer training epochs. This demonstrates that a principled physiological inductive bias can match or exceed the performance of more complex representations. Code is available at: https://github.com/BonniciJ/OrbitalEcho/

</details>


### [143] [Multimodal Taxonomic Conditioning for Generative Plankton Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11673)
*Daniela Ivanova,Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对自动浮游生物成像数据的长尾分布问题，提出以分类学为条件的合成图像生成方法：将CLIP编码器在大规模浮游生物语料上以面向深层不规则分类体系的排序对比目标进行适配，再冻结该编码器以条件化一个参数高效扩散Transformer，并从分布保真度与下游分类器效用两方面评估合成样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动浮游生物成像产生的数据集呈严重长尾分布，生态学意义上最重要的稀有类群图像过少，难以可靠地训练或评估分类器，因此需要为稀有类群补充高质量数据。

Method: 方法分两步：首先在大规模浮游生物语料上适配一个CLIP编码器，采用扩展至深层、不规则（ragged）分类体系的排序对比目标（ranked contrastive objective）；随后冻结该编码器，用以条件化一个参数高效（parameter-efficient）的扩散Transformer，从而按分类学条件生成合成浮游生物图像。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅说明评估维度：从分布保真度（distributional fidelity）与下游分类器效用（downstream classifier utility）两方面衡量合成样本质量。

Conclusion: 该工作提出以分类学条件化的扩散生成框架缓解浮游生物图像长尾问题，为稀有类群的数据增广与分类器训练提供了可行路径，其有效性需通过分布保真度和下游任务性能共同验证。

Abstract: Automated plankton imaging produces severely long-tailed datasets, where the rare taxa of greatest ecological interest have too few images to train or evaluate classifiers reliably. We generate synthetic plankton imagery conditioned on taxonomy: a CLIP encoder is adapted on a large plankton corpus with a ranked contrastive objective extended to deep, ragged taxonomies, then frozen to condition a parameter-efficient diffusion transformer. We evaluate synthetic sample quality on distributional fidelity and downstream classifier utility.

</details>


### [144] [Single-Stream Multi-Feature Fusion with Temporal Robustness for Gait Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11680)
*Shirong Lyu,Silu Quan,Yixuan Ding,Chengpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SV-GCN单流多特征融合框架，通过帧内相对运动特征与全局掩码引导的有效帧时空图卷积实现时序不变性，在E-Gait上性能媲美SOTA且跨序列长度与帧率泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D骨架步态情感识别面临标注成本高、数据稀缺、异构数据泛化差的问题；同时多流架构复杂、模型对帧率与序列长度敏感，难以利用大规模骨架动作识别数据预训练。

Method: 提出SV-GCN单流多特征融合框架：1）引入帧内相对运动特征消除帧率敏感性；2）在浅层嵌入异构线索，实现早期融合，规避多流复杂度；3）针对变长序列设计全局掩码引导的有效帧时空图卷积模块，首次在该领域引入帧率不敏感性。

Result: 在E-Gait数据集上性能与当前最优方法相当，并在不同序列长度与帧率下展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为在规模化骨架动作识别数据集上进行预训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: 3D skeleton-based gait emotion recognition faces high annotation costs, data scarcity, and poor generalization on heterogeneous data. This paper proposes SV-GCN, a single-stream multi-feature fusion framework with temporal invariance. We introduce intra-frame relative motion features to eliminate frame-rate sensitivity and embed heterogeneous cues at shallow layers, enabling early fusion without multi-stream complexity. For variable-length sequences, we design a global mask-guided valid-frame spatio-temporal graph convolution module, introducing frame-rate insensitivity for the first time in this domain. On the E-Gait dataset, our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art while demonstrating strong generalization across varying sequence lengths and frame rates, offering a viable pathway for pre-training on large-scale skeleton-based action recognition datasets.

</details>


### [145] [Spectral Adapters for Segment Anything Model-based Segmentation of Colorectal Liver Metastases in Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11703)
*Ramtin Mojtahedi,Mohammad Hamghalam,Jacob J. Peoples,Natalie Gangai,Mithat Gonen,Yun Shin Chun,HyunSeon Christine Kang,Richard K. G. Do,Amber L. Simpson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种参数高效的谱适配器（DiSECT与SiGA）用于SAM，在结直肠癌肝转移CT分割中实现与nnU-Net相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌肝转移（CRLM）在增强CT中的精确分割对疗效评估、手术规划与随访至关重要，但需要高效的模型适配方法。

Method: DiSECT通过对冻结权重的奇异值分解，将残差更新约束在前导谱方向；SiGA在此基础上通过多层感知机加入全局与输入条件门控。在446例增强CT体积（355训练/91测试）上评估，对比LoRA、QLoRA、卷积适配器（CAD）及3D nnU-Net基线，涵盖单点、三点、边界框与无提示四种模式。

Result: SiGA在单点提示下表现最佳，Dice 0.77、IoU 0.69、HD95 35.39 mm；无提示推理下Dice 0.76、IoU 0.68、HD95 46.76 mm，与nnU-Net基线（0.758 Dice）相当。DiSECT仅需0.14M可训练参数。

Conclusion: 谱适配器能以有限的可训练参数高效适配SAM用于CRLM分割，并保持强劲精度。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of colorectal liver metastases (CRLM) in contrast-enhanced computed tomography (CT) is important for response assessment, surgical planning, and follow-up. We propose two parameter-efficient spectral adapters for the Segment Anything Model (SAM): the Directional Spectral Adapter (DiSECT) and Spectral Instance-Guided Adapter (SiGA). DiSECT uses singular value decomposition of frozen weights to constrain residual updates to leading spectral directions, while SiGA adds global and input-conditioned gating through a multilayer perceptron. We evaluate these methods on 446 contrast-enhanced CT volumes (355 training, 91 testing) and compare them with LoRA, QLoRA, convolutional adapters (CAD), and a 3D nnU-Net baseline. Experiments consider single-point, three-point, bounding-box, and no-prompt regimes. SiGA achieves the best single-point performance with a Dice score of 0.77, IoU of 0.69, and HD95 of 35.39 mm. Under no-prompt inference, SiGA reaches 0.76 Dice, 0.68 IoU, and 46.76 mm HD95, comparable to the nnU-Net baseline (0.758 Dice). DiSECT uses only 0.14 million trainable parameters. These results show that spectral adapters can efficiently adapt SAM for CRLM segmentation while retaining strong accuracy with limited trainable parameters.

</details>


### [146] [Language-Augmented Semantic Priors for B-Spline Surface Fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11708)
*Yunzhong Lou,Yusheng Luo,Jiahao Li,Yu Song,Xiangdong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LASP 框架，利用大语言模型从建模历史中推断结构化 B 样条先验，作为语义推理层指导传统 CAD 拟合，提升语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 CAD 几何内核在曲面拟合与参数化中依赖预设的启发式初始化，且建模历史中的过程语义与设计意图未被利用，导致高层设计意图与求解器可执行几何配置之间存在鸿沟，拟合结果次优且语义不一致。

Method: LASP 作为现有求解器之上的语义推理层，不修改几何内核。先将建模历史翻译为包含设计意图、几何上下文与功能关系的文本描述，再用微调后的 LLM 预测结构化 B 样条先验参数；采用两阶段训练方案，融合局部几何规律与长程上下文依赖。

Result: 实验表明，相比传统机器学习方案，语言驱动的推理可作为几何求解的有效归纳偏置，生成的先验兼具可解释性与语义连贯性，引导拟合过程更准确体现设计目标。

Conclusion: 语言驱动推理为几何求解提供了强有力的归纳偏置，确立了现代 CAD 系统中语言引导几何优化的新范式。

Abstract: The use of B-splines and Non-Uniform Rational B-Splines surfaces constitutes the mathematical foundation of contemporary computer-aided design (CAD) systems. Despite long-term progress, geometric kernels in traditional CAD still rely heavily on predetermined heuristic initialization for surface fitting and parameterization. Meanwhile, the procedural semantics and design intent encoded in modeling histories are largely ignored during geometry generation. This disconnect creates a gap between high-level design intent and solver-executable geometric configuration, often leading to suboptimal and semantically inconsistent fitting results. To bridge this gap, we introduce LASP, a Language-Augmented Semantic Priors framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to infer structured, solver-usable B-spline priors from procedural modeling histories. Rather than modifying the geometric kernel itself, LASP operates as a semantic reasoning layer above existing solvers. It first translates modeling histories into rich textual descriptions that capture design intent, geometric context, and functional relationships, and then uses a fine-tuned LLM to predict structured B-spline prior parameters. LASP is trained through a two-stage scheme that combines local geometric regularities with long-range contextual dependencies, producing priors that are both interpretable and semantically coherent. This approach furnishes inductive signals that direct the conventional B-spline fitting process toward solutions that more accurately encapsulate the intended design objectives and demonstrate heightened semantic coherence. Compared to traditional machine learning schemes, the experiments demonstrate that language-driven reasoning can serve as a powerful inductive bias for geometric solving, establishing a new paradigm of language-guided geometric optimization in modern CAD systems.

</details>


### [147] [MC-DeTra: Motion-Consistent Joint Object Detection and Socially-Aware Trajectory Forecasting in Bird's-Eye-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11717)
*Vladislav Diuzhev,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开源复现了无公开实现的 DeTra 模型，并提出 MC-DeTra 运动一致性机制，通过训练期辅助监督提升共享 BEV 表示下的动态目标轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一目标检测与轨迹预测模型在自动驾驶中融合感知与预测，但对动态移动目标的预测精度仍是最难的部分；且最强的此类模型 DeTra 缺乏公开实现，难以复现与比较。

Method: 作者贡献了带文档化近似说明的开源 DeTra 复现，并在其上提出 MC-DeTra：一族运动一致性机制，通过两个由标注导出的辅助信号（目标观测到的历史运动、构成其社会背景的周围交通占据情况）以及一个输出间一致性约束（对齐预测朝向与预测运动方向）来增加监督。所有损失仅在训练阶段使用，推理安全，测试时移除，不增加推理延迟。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Dataset 上，采用严格的、以检测为条件的预测协议评估，MC-DeTra 提升了动态、具有社会情境的轨迹预测性能，同时保持或改善检测精度；基于梯度的损失校准分析揭示了辅助目标在共享骨干网络上的竞争关系，消融实验识别出贡献最大的信号。

Conclusion: MC-DeTra 通过训练期辅助监督有效改善统一模型的动态轨迹预测，且不带来推理开销；作者开源了代码、配置与评估工具以便复现和后续研究。

Abstract: Unified models for object detection and trajectory forecasting aim to merge perception and prediction for autonomous driving, refining actor trajectories directly over shared bird's-eye-view (BEV) images rasterized from LiDAR and high-definition maps. Their accuracy on dynamic, moving actors, however, remains the hardest part of the task, and the strongest such model, DeTra, has no public implementation. We contribute an openly released DeTra reimplementation with documented approximations, and on top of it MC-DeTra: a family of motion-consistency mechanisms that add supervision through two annotation-derived auxiliary signals -- each actor's observed past motion and the occupancy of the surrounding traffic that forms its social context -- and one inter-output consistency constraint that aligns an actor's predicted heading with its predicted direction of motion. Every proposed loss is train-only and inference-safe: it shapes the shared BEV representation during training and is removed at test time, adding no inference latency. On the Waymo Open Dataset, evaluated under a strict, detection-conditioned forecasting protocol, MC-DeTra improves dynamic, socially-situated trajectory forecasting while preserving or improving detection accuracy; a gradient-based loss-calibration analysis exposes how the auxiliary objectives compete at the shared backbone, and our ablation identifies which signals contribute most. We release code, configurations, and evaluation tooling at https://github.com/diuzhevVlad/MC-DeTra.

</details>


### [148] [Revisiting Avatar-As-Image: High-Fidelity Registration is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11722)
*Margaret Kostyrko,Yuxuan Xue,Garvita Tiwari,Gerard Pons-Moll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AvaImg多阶段优化管线，首次从任意着装扫描中生成高保真SMPL(-X)+D配准与UV纹理映射，误差接近扫描本身（PSNR=34.48dB），并验证其UV图可被冻结的FLUX VAE编码，处于自然图像分布内，支持2D生成先验用于3D化身生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3D着装人体表示为标准2D UV纹理与位移贴图可复用预训练图像网络进行生成与编辑，但前提是扫描需高保真配准对齐到统一模板；该前提此前从未被满足，被认为是既有UV方法质量受限的根因。同时尚无公开方法能从任意着装扫描生成高保真SMPL(-X)+D配准与UV纹理。

Method: AvaImg为多阶段优化管线：1）用有符号缠绕数（signed winding numbers）施加"身体在衣物内"约束，并通过三级效率级联使其可行（运行时间减少约10倍，存储节省约95%）；2）采用由粗到细的位移优化恢复精细表面细节。

Result: 在六个数据集上，AvaImg在人体拟合、体型估计与表面配准方面均优于所有基线；生成的带纹理配准结果与扫描近乎不可区分（PSNR=34.48dB）。通过冻结的FLUX VAE自动编码UV图，仅增加0.76mm Chamfer误差，且生成的map位于自然图像分布内，验证了Avatar-as-Image表示与图像基础模型的兼容性。

Conclusion: AvaImg填补了高保真着装人体UV配准的空白，其Avatar-as-Image表示支持2D生成先验用于3D化身生成。代码、数据与Singularity容器将在 https://yuxuan-xue.com/avaimg 公布。

Abstract: The representation of 3D clothed humans as standardized 2D UV texture and displacement maps over an underlying body model has long been studied. This compact representation is enticing as it enables pretrained image networks to process, generate, and edit 3D avatars, but is only useful if scans are accurately aligned and brought into correspondence via high-fidelity registration. This prerequisite has never been met, which we argue explains the limited quality of prior UV-based methods for clothed humans. Despite its significance, no public method produces high-fidelity SMPL(-X)+D registrations with UV texture from arbitrary clothed scans. We present AvaImg, a multi-stage optimization pipeline, to close this gap: it enforces body-inside-clothing constraint via signed winding numbers, made viable by a three-level efficiency cascade (~10x runtime reduced, ~95% storage saved), and recovers fine surface detail using coarse-to-fine displacement optimization. AvaImg outperforms all baselines in body fitting, shape estimation, and surface registration across six datasets, yielding textured registrations near-indistinguishable from scans (PSNR=34.48dB). For validation of AvaImg's Avatar-as-Image representation as imminently compatible with image foundation models, we auto-encode our UV maps via the frozen FLUX VAE. This achieves only 0.76mm added Chamfer error relative to scan and shows that the resulting maps lie within natural-image distributions, supporting the use of 2D generative priors for 3D avatar generation. Code, data, and Singularity containers will be at https://yuxuan-xue.com/avaimg.

</details>


### [149] [Logit Refiner: Improving Visual Autoregressive Models via Intra-Scale Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11804)
*Meimingwei Li,Stefan Andreas Baumann,Felix Krause,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出VAR的并行解码本质上是平均场近似，丢弃了同尺度token的空间依赖，导致局部不连贯；提出轻量级Logit Refiner恢复尺度内依赖，仅增加约10%参数即可显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR通过下一尺度预测生成图像，在同一尺度内并行生成所有token。作者认为这种并行解码是一种平均场式近似，忽略了同一尺度token之间的空间依赖关系，因此无论骨干模型容量多大，都会产生局部不连贯的样本——这是解码规则本身的局限，而非模型能力问题。

Method: 提出Logit Refiner：一个轻量级自回归模块，在冻结的骨干特征条件下，以序列方式对token进行采样，从而恢复尺度内依赖关系。该模块仅增加约10%参数、不到基础模型5%的训练算力，可直接插入任意预训练VAR检查点，无需重新训练。通过受控消融实验，分离出"联合尺度内采样"这一关键因素。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256类别条件生成任务上，覆盖310M至2B参数的多种骨干，Refiner均能持续提升生成质量，使1.1B参数模型超越其两倍规模的模型。该方法还可推广到文本到图像生成，证明确实存在平均场瓶颈且可被有效缓解。

Conclusion: VAR并行解码的平均场瓶颈是解码规则的固有局限，跨VAR变体普遍存在；通过轻量级的尺度内自回归精炼即可有效缓解，且具有良好通用性与可插拔性。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive Models (VAR) generate images through next-scale prediction, producing all tokens within each scale in parallel. We show that this parallel decoding constitutes a mean-field-style approximation that discards spatial dependencies among same-scale tokens, causing locally incoherent samples regardless of backbone capacity -- a limitation of the decoding rule. Addressing this limitation, we introduce the Logit Refiner, a lightweight autoregressive module that restores intra-scale dependencies by sequentially sampling tokens conditioned on frozen backbone features. Adding only ~10% parameters and less than 5% of the base model's training compute, it plugs into any pretrained VAR checkpoint without retraining. Controlled ablations isolate joint intra-scale sampling -- rather than additional capacity or training -- as the critical ingredient. Across backbones from 310M to 2B parameters on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256, the refiner consistently improves generation quality, enabling a 1.1B-parameter model to surpass one twice its size. The approach further generalizes to text-to-image generation, confirming that the mean-field bottleneck persists across VAR variants and is effectively alleviated by our method.
  Project page: https://compvis.github.io/logit-refiner/

</details>


### [150] [Guided Super-Resolution of Digital Elevation Models with Diffusion-Based Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11886)
*Armand Mihai Nicolicioiu,Dominik Narnhofer,Nando Metzger,Daniel Panangian,Ksenia Bittner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于去噪扩散的引导式超分辨率方法，利用高分辨率光谱影像将5 m DSM增强至0.5 m，实现更准确的城市地表细节重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率数字表面模型（DSM）在城市分析、三维建筑重建与基础设施监测中至关重要，但数据获取成本高、流程复杂，导致其可得性受限；而商业卫星提供的粗分辨率DSM广泛可获取，高分辨率光学影像也日益丰富，二者之间存在显著的空间分辨率不匹配问题。

Method: 采用去噪扩散（denoising diffusion）框架进行引导式DSM超分辨率：以5 m DSM为输入，高分辨率光谱影像作为引导，将仅可见于影像中的信息（如清晰轮廓与精细屋顶结构）迁移到高程图中，从而把DSM提升至0.5 m分辨率。

Result: 在中欧多个城市的实验中，所提方法生成的DSM质量较高，结构细节得到改善，表面几何更为准确，优于传统插值或滤波技术。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合基础图像先验的引导式超分辨率是重建高分辨率地表模型的有效途径，可缓解高分辨率DSM数据稀缺的问题。

Abstract: High-resolution digital surface models (DSMs) play an important role in urban analysis, 3D building reconstruction, and infrastructure monitoring, yet their availability remains limited due to the high cost and complexity of data acquisition. In contrast, coarse DSMs from commercial satellite missions are widely accessible, and high-resolution optical imagery is increasingly available from aerial and satellite platforms. We address the resulting mismatch in spatial resolution and propose a DSM superresolution approach that enhances 5 m DSMs to 0.5 m resolution, using guidance from high-resolution spectral images. Our method employs denoising diffusion to transfer information that is visible only in the image, like crisp outlines and detailed roof structures, into the elevation maps. In this way, surface details are reconstructed more accurately than with conventional interpolation or filtering techniques. Experiments on several cities in Central Europe demonstrate that the proposed approach produces high-quality DSMs with improved structural detail and accurate surface geometry. Our results highlight the potential of guided super-resolution with foundational image priors as a means of reconstructing high-resolution surface models.

</details>


### [151] [3D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11894)
*Adnan Armouti,Yixuan Gao,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D点泼溅（3DPS），首个基于雷达方程实体角形式的可微点渲染器，输出复值信号，同时实现物理保真、复值与多视角可训练，训练快且精度显著优于光学NVS迁移基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达新视角合成（NVS）要求渲染器同时具备三个属性：物理保真、复值输出、多视角可训练，但现有方法无法兼得。可微蒙特卡洛光线追踪虽直接实现雷达前向模型（显式材质建模、复值输出），却难以扩展到NVS所需的多视角优化；而NeRF、哈希网格、3D高斯等光学NVS迁移方法训练快，却丢弃相位、以不透明学习特征替代显式材质建模，仅能产生功率型距离-方位（RA）幅度。

Method: 提出3D Point Splatting（3DPS），首个雷达可微点渲染器，直接由雷达方程的标准立体角形式推导。每个有向3D点携带ITU-R P.2040材质模型并以闭式求解，产生的复相量通过预计算点扩散函数（PSF）泼溅到距离bin中。复值输出使渲染器与产品格式无关：同一优化场景可经标准FFT流程得到ADC、复距离像（CRP）与RA输出，无需针对每种格式重训。

Result: 在六个室外ColoRadar场景上，3DPS在留出RA图像上达到0.587的平均Pearson相关，是三个光学NVS基线（RadarSplat、Radar Fields、DART）的1.7倍至5.2倍。单个RTX 4090上每场景训练约3分钟。

Conclusion: 3DPS首次在单一可微渲染器中同时满足物理保真、复值输出与多视角可训练三重要求，并通过闭式材质模型与PSF泼溅实现高效的格式无关雷达NVS，在精度与训练效率上均优于光学NVS迁移方案。

Abstract: Solving novel view synthesis (NVS) for millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar requires a renderer that is physically faithful, complex-valued, and multi-viewpoint-tractable. No prior method achieves these three properties simultaneously. Differentiable Monte Carlo (MC) ray tracers implement the radar forward model directly with explicit material modeling and complex outputs, but do not scale to the multi-view optimization NVS demands. Optical-NVS ports of NeRF, hash grids, and 3D Gaussians train fast but discard phase and replace explicit material modeling with opaque learned features, restricting them to power-only range-azimuth (RA) magnitudes. We propose 3D Point Splatting (3DPS), the first differentiable point renderer for radar, derived directly from the standard solid-angle form of the radar equation. Each oriented 3D point carries an ITU-R P.2040 material model, evaluated in closed form, with the resulting complex phasor splatted into range bins through a precomputed point spread function (PSF). The complex-valued output makes the renderer product-agnostic. The same optimized scene yields analog-to-digital converter (ADC), complex range profile (CRP), and RA outputs through standard fast Fourier transform (FFT) pipelines without retraining for each format. On six outdoor ColoRadar scenes, 3DPS reaches 0.587 mean Pearson correlation on held-out RA images. This is between 1.7x and 5.2x the three optical-NVS baselines (RadarSplat, Radar Fields, DART). Training takes approximately 3 minutes per scene on a single RTX 4090.

</details>


### [152] [Caption-once, Frames-on-Demand: Visual-Need Routing for Budget-Aware Agentic Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11899)
*Weitong Cai,Hang Zhang,Yukai Huang,Yiqiao Xie,Shan Gao,Jiankang Deng,Songcen Xu,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Caption-once, Frames-on-Demand (CFD)，一种面向边缘设备长视频理解的预算感知边缘-云协同智能体框架，通过"文本记忆承载时序结构、视觉像素按需调取"的双轨设计显著降低在线视觉处理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的长视频理解需要在严格算力与带宽预算下推理数小时内容。现有方案存在两难：对视觉 token 做下采样会破坏时序结构；纯文本视频记忆则丢失细粒度视觉属性。作者观察到"视觉-文本对偶性"：语言记忆比密集帧更能承载长程时序结构，而像素对属性级感知仍不可替代。

Method: 提出 CFD 框架。边缘端仅执行一次离线 captioning，构建双轨叙事索引——事件级故事骨架（story skeleton）与片段级微日志（micro-log），并缓存复用，跨查询无需重新 captioning。查询时云端 MLLM 以"故事优先"循环推理该索引，核心是轻量级 Visual-Need Router：逐查询门控模块，仅对感知类问题（外观、屏上文字、属性消歧）触发有界关键帧检索，而时序-结构类问题保留在语言空间。该路由把视觉访问变为一等、查询条件化的成本项，无论视频多长都限制每查询的帧消耗。

Result: 在长视频基准上展示了良好的精度-效率权衡，同时大幅减少在线视觉处理量。

Conclusion: 通过将长程时序推理交由语言记忆、将视觉检索限制为按需触发的有界关键帧，CFD 在边缘-云预算约束下实现了高效且准确的长视频理解。

Abstract: Long-video understanding on edge devices must reason over hours of content under tight compute and bandwidth budgets. Subsampling visual tokens loses temporal structure, while text-only video memories lose fine-grained visual attributes. We observe a visual-textual duality: language memories carry long-range temporal structure better than dense frames, while pixels remain decisive for attribute-level perception. Building on this insight, we propose Caption-once, Frames-onDemand (CFD), a budget-aware edge-cloud agentic framework. The edge runs a single offline captioning pass that builds a dual-track narrative index, an event-level story skeleton plus a clip-level micro-log, cached and reused across queries without re-captioning. At query time, a cloud-side MLLM reasons over the index in a story-first loop centered on a lightweight Visual-Need Router: a per-query gating module that triggers bounded keyframe retrieval only for perceptual questions (appearance, on-screen text, attribute disambiguation) and keeps temporal-structural questions in language space. The router turns visual access into a first-class, query-conditioned cost, capping per-query frame consumption regardless of video length. Experiments on long-video benchmarks demonstrate strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs while substantially reducing online visual processing.

</details>


### [153] [SenseNova-U1.5: Towards Native Unified Visual Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11929)
*Haiwen Diao,Jiahao Wang,Chenjing Ding,Hanming Deng,Jiangnan Chen,Ruixi Zhang,Ruohui Wang,Wenwen Tong,Xiangyu Fan,Yubo Wang,Yue Zhu,Yuwei Niu,Zhengqi Bai,Zhiqian Lin,Zhitao Yang,Zhongang Cai,Bo Yang,Chen Feng,Chengguang Lv,Guangjia Liu,Guanlin Wang,Hanyu Zhang,Haojia Yu,Hongcan Xiao,Hongli Wang,Huan Wu,Huaping Zhong,Jian Fang,Jianan Fan,Jiaqi Li,Jiefan Lu,Jing Zuo,Jingcheng Ni,Junxiang Xu,Linjun Dai,Mutian Xu,Peishen Yan,Penghao Wu,Ruijie Mao,Ruisi Wang,Shihao Bai,Shuang Yang,Shuya Yang,Shuyan Zheng,Silei Wu,Siying Li,Tao Chu,Tianbo Zhong,Tongxi Zhou,Weichao Luo,Weichen Fan,Wenhao Jia,Wenjie Gao,Xiangli Kong,Yan Li,Yang Yong,Zimo Wen,Zixuan Qian,Wenxiu Sun,Ruihao Gong,Quan Wang,Lewei Lu,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseNova-U1.5 是一个 8B MoT 原生统一多模态模型，在无编码器、无 VAE 架构下统一理解、推理与视觉生成，通过视觉接口强化、数据与任务优化及多专家在线策略蒸馏显著提升图像保真度、文本渲染、复杂构图与编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型多依赖外部视觉编码器与 VAE，架构割裂、端到端性不足；同时视觉生成在保真度、文本渲染、复杂构图、多参考编辑与交错生成等方面仍有提升空间，需要探索真正端到端、理解与生成贯通的建模路径。

Method: 提出 8B-MoT 原生统一多模态模型 SenseNova-U1.5，采用无编码器、无 VAE 架构；通过空间连贯的 patch 重建强化视觉接口；以精心筛选的生成与编辑数据扩展训练，改进任务形式化、引入结构化提示增强，支持最高 4K 原生分辨率；后训练阶段针对视觉美学、双语文本渲染、信息图生成与图像编辑训练专用专家，并通过多专家在线策略蒸馏整合能力。

Result: 在广泛评测中，SenseNova-U1.5 在图像保真度、文本渲染、复杂构图、多参考编辑与交错生成方面大幅提升，同时改善指令遵循，并更好地保持主体身份、几何结构与未修改区域；尽管生成数据中结构化格式曝光有限，模型仍能有效泛化到长、复杂、结构化的视觉指令。

Conclusion: 结果表明多模态理解能力可迁移至视觉规划与创作，原生统一建模是通向感知、推理与创作一体化端到端系统的可行路径；作者将开源训练代码，包括监督微调、强化学习与在线策略蒸馏。

Abstract: We launch SenseNova-U1.5, an 8B-MoT native unified multimodal model that understands, reasons about, and generates visual content within an encoder-free and VAE-free architecture. We strengthen its visual interface through spatially coherent patch reconstruction and scale its training with carefully curated generation and editing data, improved task formulation, structural prompt enhancement, and native resolutions of up to 4K. For post-training, we optimize specialized experts for visual aesthetics, bilingual text rendering, infographic generation, and image editing, and consolidate their capabilities through multi-expert on-policy distillation. Across extensive evaluations, SenseNova-U1.5 largely advances image fidelity, text rendering, complex composition, multi-reference editing, and interleaved generation, while improving instruction following and preserving subject identity, geometry, and unmodified regions. Despite limited exposure to structured formats in its generation data, SenseNova-U1.5 generalizes effectively to long, complex, and structured visual instructions, further proving that multimodal understanding can transfer to visual planning and creation. Together, these findings position native unified modelling as a promising path towards systems that perceive, reason and create within a fully end-to-end framework. We will open-source training code, including supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

### [154] [An Empirical Measurement of Jailbreaking Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10594)
*Yujie Mu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Expert evaluation of jailbreak responses is costly and difficult to scale, so the community increasingly relies on automated evaluators to determine whether an attack succeeds. However, jailbreak studies typically validate their chosen evaluator independently, repeatedly spending resources on similar evaluation efforts while making results across papers difficult to compare. Different evaluators also encode different definitions of jailbreak success, meaning that reported attack strength and apparent progress can depend substantially on which evaluator is used. We systematically compare six evaluators that recur in recent jailbreak attack and defense research: HarmBench, JailbreakBench, JailbreakRadar, StrongReject, JADES, and JailMeter. To our knowledge, no prior study has evaluated all six on the same human-labeled data under a controlled setup. We evaluate them on JailbreakQR and JailMeter-Eva, using human judgments as the reference, and measure agreement with humans, error types, and consistency across attack families. For evaluators that require a general-purpose LLM judge, we use a shared backbone to control for model-specific variation. We found that JADES exhibits the best overall performance, while HarmBench and StrongReject also demonstrate good performance.

</details>


### [155] [Threshold Choice, Not Sample Size, Bounds Trustless Verification of Nondeterministic Compound AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10601)
*Alper Alimoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向跨边缘-云-轨道、无单一所有者的复合AI流水线的复现验证协议，同时容忍输出非确定性、执行节点可能不诚实、以及共享记录间歇可访问这三个挑战；核心发现是：在固定的重执行离散度与指标下，真正约束检出能力的是判定阈值而非样本数k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI流水线（LLM调用、检索器、工具链）具有非确定性，且日益运行于无人单一拥有的异构节点上；这些节点的优化会在被检查前丢弃中间结果。在该环境下验证"复现"需要同时应对三件事：容忍非确定输出、容忍不诚实节点、容忍对共享记录的间歇访问，而已有工作最多只能同时覆盖其中两项，故作者希望用一个协议覆盖全部三点，并明确其失效边界。

Method: 协议在每一阶段对输入、输出与上下文计算摘要并提交，配合一个策略摘要钉住指标与阈值；这些承诺在不信任执行节点的前提下被锚定，在分区时延后处理，并以k次重执行的中位数来裁决挑战，无需仲裁者（no quorum）。随后在合成的HotpotQA流水线上进行校准与对抗评估，比较固定阈值、按每次执行导出的阈值，以及在固定重执行离散度下不同k和阈值的表现。

Result: 在合成HotpotQA流水线上，一个校准过的固定阈值在45次诚实复现中接受44次、在105对分歧配对中拒绝104对，但在k=5时仍有29次试验中的27次放行"同输入伪造"，且增加样本数无助于事（采样只会锐化估计而不移动它）。在固定该指标与流水线重执行离散度后，绑定的约束是阈值而非样本量：按每次执行导出的阈值在29次中检出19次，而匹配到相同零诚实拒绝率的最佳常数阈值仅达9次；该规则在k=5时不拒绝任何诚实承诺（k=3时为15次中3次），并能捕获针对它构建的攻击者的15次中的11次，因为攻击者需瞄准一个在其承诺产生之后才抽取的目标。该规则仅被测量，尚未部署。

Conclusion: 该协议首次同时覆盖非确定输出、不诚实节点与间歇性共享记录访问三个挑战；实证表明在给定指标与重执行离散度下，样本量k不是瓶颈，阈值选择才是决定性的约束，且按每次执行自适应导出的阈值显著优于最佳常数阈值。作者将该规则定位为经测量但尚未部署的方案。

Abstract: Compound AI pipelines chain LLM calls, retrievers, and tools and are nondeterministic: sampling, model updates, and volatile tool responses make one input yield different outputs across runs. Such pipelines increasingly run across edge, cloud, and orbital nodes owned by no single party, whose optimizations discard intermediate results before inspection. Verifying reproduction there means tolerating nondeterministic outputs, a node that may not report honestly, and intermittent access to any shared record; existing work addresses at most two at once. We give a protocol covering all three: it commits digests of each stage's inputs, outputs, and context under a policy digest pinning the metric and threshold, anchors them without trusting the executing node, defers under partition, and decides a challenge on the median of $k$ re-executions, with no quorum. Where that procedure breaks is the main result. On a synthetic HotpotQA pipeline a calibrated fixed threshold accepts 44 of 45 honest reproductions and rejects 104 of 105 divergent pairs, yet lets same-input fabrication through in 27 of 29 trials at k=5, more samples being no help since sampling sharpens an estimate without moving it. Holding that metric and this pipeline's re-execution spread fixed, the binding constraint is the threshold rather than the sample size: one derived per execution detects 19 of 29 where the best constant matched to the same zero honest rejections reaches 9, rejects no honest commitment at k=5 though 3 of 15 at k=3, and catches 11 of 15 of an attacker built against it, which has to aim at a target drawn only after its commitment exists. That rule is measured rather than deployed.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Diffusion Freezing: Privacy-preserving Diffusion Models Against Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10608)
*Jialu Guo,Xiao Han,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出自适应扩散冻结（ADF）框架，通过跨时间步自适应冻结与风险感知掩码策略，在隐私、效用与效率之间取得更优权衡，有效抵御扩散模型的成员推理攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型生成能力出色，但训练过程存在显著隐私隐患，尤其易受成员推理攻击（MIA）。已有隐私保护方法难以同时兼顾隐私、效用与效率，存在明显权衡缺口。

Method: 1）跨时间步自适应冻结训练：通过掩码矩阵显式控制不同数据子集在各扩散时间步的参与，减少过度记忆，使成员与非成员样本的模型行为更趋一致；2）基于预训练的风险感知冻结策略：依据记忆倾向估计MIA风险，抑制高风险"子集-时间步"对的贡献，在降低成员泄漏的同时尽量不损害生成质量。

Result: 在多个数据集上的评估表明，ADF具备有效的防御性能，相比多种基线方法实现了当前最优的隐私-效用-效率权衡表现。

Conclusion: ADF通过掩码式的跨时间步冻结与风险感知策略，有效缓解MIA，并兼顾生成质量与训练效率，为隐私保护扩散模型提供了更优的权衡方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative tasks across various areas, however their training process raises significant privacy concerns, particularly under membership inference attacks (MIAs). Prior studies on privacy-preserving of diffusion models fail to balance privacy, utility, and efficiency. To address this gap, we propose a novel framework of privacy-preserving diffusion models, Adaptive Diffusion Freezing (ADF), which can defend against MIAs with better trade-off. By leveraging cross-timestep adaptive freezing training, ADF explicitly control the participation of different data subsets across diffusion timesteps via a mask matrix, which reduces the over-memorization and leads to more uniform model behaviors between member and nonmember samples. To construct a freezing mask matrix that effectively reduce membership leakage without unnecessarily harming generation quality, we introduce a pretraining-based risk-aware freezing policy to estimate MIA risk based on memorization tendency, and suppress the contribution of the subset-timestep pairs with higher risk. Evaluations on multiple datasets demonstrate that ADF provides effective defense performance as well as state-of-the-art privacy-utility-efficiency trade-off performance compared to various baselines.

</details>


### [157] [Black-Box Membership Inference via Word-Level Probability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10611)
*Shengjie Niu,Yeheng Ge,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出WPMIA，一种仅依赖文本续写的严格黑盒成员推理攻击方法，通过蒙特卡洛采样与局部核平滑估计词级生成概率并聚合为序列级似然，在多种开源及专有LLM上优于现有黑盒基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推理攻击（MIAs）多依赖逐token的logits或概率，无法应用于仅暴露文本续写的专有LLM；严格黑盒场景下的隐私审计需求尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出词级概率MIA（WPMIA）：通过蒙特卡洛采样结合局部核平滑估计词级生成概率，聚合成序列级似然估计；并以不同前缀为条件构造条件似然，放大成员与非成员之间的分布差异，实现严格黑盒审计。

Result: 在多个开源LLM上持续优于现有黑盒基线；在GPT-5-Chat、Gemini-2.5-Flash和Claude-4.5-Haiku等现代专有LLM上平均达到42.0的TPR@5%FPR。

Conclusion: WPMIA为严格黑盒成员推理提供了统计学上合理的基础，为未来相关研究奠定坚实起点。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have emerged as critical tools for auditing privacy risks in large language models (LLMs), aiming to determine whether a given text was included in a model's training corpus. However, most existing MIAs require access to per-token logits or probabilities, making them inapplicable in practice to proprietary LLMs that expose only textual continuations. To address this underexplored setting, we propose Word-level Probability MIA (WPMIA), a statistically principled MIA for strict black-box privacy auditing. WPMIA estimates word-level generation probabilities via Monte Carlo sampling with local kernel smoothing, then aggregates these estimates into a sequence-level likelihood estimator. Furthermore, WPMIA constructs the likelihood conditioned on different prefixes, thereby amplifying the distributional differences between members and non-members. We evaluate WPMIA across various open-source LLMs and find that it consistently outperforms existing black-box baselines. Importantly, we also evaluate WPMIA on modern proprietary LLMs, including GPT-5-Chat, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Claude-4.5-Haiku, achieving an average TPR@5\%FPR of 42.0 across these models. These results offer a sound foundation for future research on strict black-box membership inference. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}.

</details>


### [158] [PEARL: A Task-Aware Framework for Evaluating Differentially Private Synthetic Educational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10612)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PEARL审计框架，对差分隐私合成教育数据同时进行有效性、隐私性、预测有用性和任务适用性四类检查；96个研究设定中仅12个通过全部检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化学习系统依赖含表现、行为与人口统计信息的真实学习者数据，此类数据高度隐私敏感。差分隐私合成数据可在降低个体暴露风险的同时支持系统开发与教育研究。但现有评估将隐私保护与预测有用性分开考察，未判断合成学习者数据对既定个性化学习任务是否仍然可用。

Method: 提出PEARL（Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger），仅当差分隐私合成教育数据集通过有效性、隐私保护、预测有用性及面向既定教育任务的适用性等全部必需检查时才予以批准，并记录每个被拒数据集的具体失败原因。在96个研究设定（由数据集、数据生成方法、隐私预算和随机种子定义）上进行评估，并开展公平性分析与知识追踪模型测试。

Result: 96个研究设定中仅12个生成的合成数据集通过全部适用的PEARL检查。许多受隐私保护的数据集因遗漏重要结果群体（如退课学生）或未能保持学习活动顺序而被拒。公平性分析显示，部分通过隐私与预测有用性检查的数据集，在按残疾与社经背景划分的群体间仍产生不均衡的高风险预测性能。此外，Deep Knowledge Tracing与Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing未能从任何被测合成知识追踪数据集中学到有意义的下一作答模式。

Conclusion: 仅靠隐私保护并不能保证合成数据对退学预测、知识追踪或自适应辅导等任务有用；合成教育数据的发布需通过任务导向的多维审计，任务适用性与群体公平性应作为与隐私同等的准入条件。

Abstract: Personalized learning systems rely on real learner data, including performance, behavior, and demographic information, but these data are highly privacy-sensitive. Differentially private (DP) synthetic data can support system development and educational research while reducing exposure of individual learners. Existing evaluations, however, assess privacy and predictive usefulness separately, without determining whether synthetic learner data remain usable for the intended personalized learning task. We introduce PEARL (Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger), which approves a DP synthetic educational dataset only when it passes all required checks of validity, privacy protection, predictive usefulness, and suitability for the intended educational task, while recording why each rejected dataset fails. Across 96 study settings, each defined by a dataset, data-generation method, privacy budget, and random seed, only 12 produced synthetic datasets that passed all applicable PEARL checks. Many privacy-protected datasets were rejected for omitting important outcome groups, such as withdrawn students, or for failing to preserve the order of learning activities. Fairness analysis further showed that some datasets passing privacy and predictive-usefulness checks still yielded unequal at-risk prediction performance across groups defined by disability and socioeconomic background. Moreover, Deep Knowledge Tracing and Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing learned no meaningful next-response patterns from any tested synthetic knowledge-tracing dataset, showing that privacy protection alone does not guarantee usefulness for dropout prediction, knowledge tracing, or adaptive tutoring.

</details>


### [159] [Understanding In-Context Multimodal Jailbreaks via Posterior Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10613)
*Xu Zhang,Dev Mistry,Xiang Xu,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出“后验重加权”框架来解释多模态大模型中的上下文学习越狱现象，并据此设计了一种推理时防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究虽观察到ICL越狱现象，但缺乏原则性理论解释，未能说明其为何可靠成功以及效果如何随上下文组成变化而扩展。

Method: 将安全对齐的MLLM建模为在竞争性行为模式上隐式运作，把上下文示例视为推理时证据，动态调整模型在安全行为与有害行为之间的后验偏好；据此形式化越狱为证据累积过程，并推导出随示例数量、有害比例、对抗强度与语义多样性变化的预测性缩放律；基于该框架提出后验感知的推理时防御，按估计风险自适应注入良性反证据。

Result: 所提防御在固定干预预算下相比现有上下文防御方法，显著改善了鲁棒性与效用之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 后验重加权可作为一个统一且具预测性的框架，用于理解并缓解MLLM中的ICL越狱问题。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) jailbreaks reveal a critical vulnerability in multimodal large language models (MLLMs): harmful demonstrations in the prompt can induce unsafe outputs without modifying model parameters. Despite extensive empirical evidence, existing work lacks a principled understanding of why such jailbreaks reliably succeed or how their effectiveness scales with context composition. We propose a posterior reweighting framework that models a safety-aligned MLLM as implicitly operating over competing behavioral modes, and interprets in-context demonstrations as inference-time evidence that dynamically shifts the model's posterior preference between safe and harmful behaviors. This view formalizes jailbreak as a process of evidence accumulation, yielding predictive scaling laws with respect to demonstration count, harmful ratio, adversarial strength, and semantic diversity. Guided by this framework, we introduce a posterior-aware inference-time defense that adaptively injects benign counter-evidence based on estimated risk, effectively suppressing harmful posterior drift while preserving model utility. Compared to existing in-context defenses, our method achieves a significantly improved robustness-utility trade-off under a fixed intervention budget. Together, our results establish posterior reweighting as a unifying and predictive framework for understanding and mitigating ICL jailbreak in MLLMs.

</details>


### [160] [SoK: Privacy Attacks on Machine Learning via Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10627)
*Abdullah Caglar Oksuz,Anisa Halimi,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 系统化梳理25项"解释泄露"研究：将模型知识与解释获取渠道分离，归纳五条获释路径，评估解释对模型提取、成员推理、模型反演（含属性推理）的威胁，并比较威胁模型、信号、指标与防御，主张按端到端披露问题来评估解释隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习解释在揭示模型行为的同时，为模型机密性与数据隐私制造了攻击面。现有研究常仅以黑盒/白盒二元标签刻画攻击者，掩盖了解释信号在"能否到达攻击者、以何种方式到达"上的本质差异，因而无法准确刻画风险来源与防御匹配问题。

Method: 对25篇利用解释进行攻击的研究进行系统化综述（systematization）。核心分析框架：（1）将"模型知识"与"解释获取方式"解耦，提出五种解释获取路径——目标方主动发布（target-released）、攻击者自行推导（attacker-derived）、二次披露（secondary disclosure）、特权访问（privileged access）、发布的全局工件（released global artifacts）；（2）把属性推理视为模型反演的部分情形统一处理；（3）沿各路径比较系统与威胁模型、解释信号类型、辅助知识、目标模型、数据模态、查询预算、评价指标、报告性能及防御措施。

Result: 跨路径证据显示：解释可降低模型提取成本；通过解释统计量、再磋商距离（recourse distance）与解释引导的鲁棒性暴露成员信号；并支持对私有输入进行空间或代数重建。比较分析得出两点否证性结论——没有任何一类解释方法是一致不安全的，也没有任何防御是一致有效的。

Conclusion: 解释隐私风险并非由"是否可解释"决定，而取决于四个耦合因素：暴露的是哪种信号、信号通过何种路径获取、攻击目标对应哪类资产、攻击者已掌握何种先验知识。因此应将解释隐私作为端到端披露问题来评估，并使防御机制与具体获取路径和被保护资产相匹配，而非寻求普适的解释安全或通用防御。

Abstract: Machine learning explanations reveal model behavior beyond predictions, creating attack surfaces for model confidentiality and data privacy. We systematize 25 studies that exploit explanations for model extraction, membership inference, and model inversion, treating attribute inference as partial inversion. Existing work is often labeled only black- or white-box, obscuring substantial differences in what explanation signal reaches an adversary. We therefore separate model knowledge from explanation acquisition and identify five paths: target-released, attacker-derived, secondary disclosure, privileged access, and released global artifacts. Across these paths, explanations reduce extraction cost, expose membership signals through explanation statistics, recourse distance, and explanation-guided robustness, and support spatial or algebraic reconstruction of private inputs. We compare system and threat models, explanation signals, auxiliary knowledge, target models, modalities, query budgets, evaluation metrics, reported performance, and defenses. Our analysis shows that no explanation family is uniformly unsafe and no defense is uniformly effective. Risk depends on which signal is exposed, how it is acquired, which asset is targeted, and what the attacker already knows. We argue that explanation privacy should therefore be evaluated as an end-to-end disclosure problem, with defenses matched to the acquisition path and protected asset.

</details>


### [161] [From Cycle Space to Cycle Manifold: Limits and Achievability of Blind False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10631)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文揭示：在直流支路潮流模型下，无噪声正交检验的残差敏感子空间恰为加权循环空间，其正交补即完整隐蔽攻击空间；加权循环空间知识是完整盲FDIA的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有盲FDIA依赖低秩测量子空间的代数视角，未能阐明使攻击隐蔽的物理电网约束，也未给出恢复完整攻击空间所需的最小信息。本文旨在从物理层面刻画隐蔽攻击的结构条件与恢复所需信息。

Method: 基于连通直流支路潮流模型，分析无噪声正交检验的残差敏感子空间结构；证明其等于加权循环空间，其正交补为完整隐蔽攻击空间。据此给出可识别性结论，并提出计算上无约束的基准方法及仅用测量的可解重建方法。进一步以紧凑交流扩展，用循环流形刻画可行支路P/Q测量，在GPU上实现拓扑辅助的流形拟合与测量生成。

Result: 理论证明加权循环空间对完整盲FDIA既必要又充分；该空间仅能将拓扑识别到2-同构，相对循环边参数在每个双连通分量上仅可识别到一个尺度，桥参数既不可识别也不需要。IEEE系统实验中在95%标称接受阈值下比较BDD绕过率与状态影响。交流扩展中可在GPU上完成流形拟合与测量生成；无损定压小角度极限下，有功功率切片的法空间退化为直流加权循环空间。

Conclusion: 加权循环空间为直流模型下隐蔽FDIA提供了完备的物理刻画与最小信息需求，并自然延伸到交流测量的循环流形框架，为盲FDIA的构造与防御提供了新的几何与拓扑视角。

Abstract: A false data injection attack (FDIA) can change the estimated grid state while evading a residual-based bad data detector (BDD). Existing blind attacks learn a low-rank measurement subspace, but this algebraic view does not state the physical grid constraints that make an attack stealthy or the minimum information needed to recover the complete attack space. Under the connected direct-current (DC) branch-flow model, we show that the residual-sensitive subspace of the noiseless orthogonal test is exactly the weighted cycle space. Its orthogonal complement is therefore the complete stealthy attack space, making weighted cycle-space knowledge both necessary and sufficient for complete blind FDIA. This space identifies the topology only up to 2-isomorphism and the relative cycle-edge parameters only up to one scale per biconnected component; bridge parameters are neither identified nor required. We then formulate a computationally unconstrained benchmark and a tractable measurement-only reconstruction method. Experiments on IEEE systems compare BDD bypass rate at a 95% nominal-acceptance threshold against state impact. As a compact alternating-current (AC) extension, we characterize feasible branch P/Q measurements by a cycle manifold and demonstrate topology-assisted manifold fitting and measurement generation on a graphics processing unit (GPU). In the lossless fixed-voltage small-angle limit, the normal space of the active-power slice reduces to the DC weighted cycle space.

</details>


### [162] [HermiCache: Enclave-Aware Cache Replacement for Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10634)
*Oussama Elmnaouri,Pascal Cotret,Vianney Lapôtre,Loïc Lagadec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 HermiCache：一种面向 RISC-V 核心的缓存侧信道防护方案，基于 Keystone TEE，在 CVA6 核心上实现，提供细粒度可配置且确定性的保护，面积开销仅 6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE 虽能保护 enclave 内存免受不可信软件访问，但由于共享微架构资源，仍易受基于缓存的侧信道攻击。现有对策如缓存划分或随机化，在需要细粒度配置和确定性保护时不理想。

Method: 提出 HermiCache，面向 RISC-V 核心设计，并在 OpenHwGroup CVA6 核心上实现，软件层采用 Keystone TEE。

Result: 该方案在处理器核心上带来 6% 的面积开销。

Conclusion: HermiCache 满足了细粒度配置与确定性保护的需求，以较低的面积代价（6%）为 RISC-V TEE 提供缓存侧信道防护。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) protect enclave memory from untrusted software but remain vulnerable to cache-based side-channel attacks due to shared microarchitectural resources. Existing countermeasures use techniques such as cache partitioning or randomization: these solutions are not ideal if a designer wants fine-grained configurations and a deterministic protection. In this paper, we introduce HermiCache which is an answer to these requirements. HermiCache is designed for RISC-V cores and has been implemented in the OpenHwGroup CVA6 core with a Keystone TEE for the software layer. The solution has an area overhead of 6% on the processor core.

</details>


### [163] [Architecting the Secure AI-SOC: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Pipeline Integrity and Threat Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10707)
*Anna Gazani,Spyridon Kounoupidis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Nikolaos Kekatos,Grigorios Tsoumakas,Georgios Koutidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号深度防御架构，通过SIEM确定性预过滤与NeMo Guardrails语义校验双層防护，阻断日志投毒引发的间接提示注入"提示件"杀伤链，并集成闭环遥测与HITL可视性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM融入SOC虽提升威胁情报处理效率，但引入日志投毒导致的间接提示注入漏洞，攻击者可嵌入恶意载荷劫持LLM操作逻辑，形成多步"提示件"杀伤链。现有防御存在两难：确定性防御计算高效但语义盲区，纯神经评估则延迟高且存在概率性缺陷。

Method: 神经符号纵深防御架构：第一层用定制SIEM解码器作确定性预过滤，在摄取边缘进行结构化净化，中和体积填充与基于签名的注入；第二层用NeMo Guardrails对结构化SIEM告警做自检式语义边界校验后再送LLM处理；并集成闭环遥测系统，在SOC仪表盘提供HITL可见性。

Result: 基于MITRE ATLAS分类法设计实验评估，针对多种提示注入测试，结果表明该协同方法能有效拆解提示件杀伤链，以可验证约束限制LLM随机性。

Conclusion: 该框架为下一代AI-SOC提供了一种弹性强、可观测性高的防御机制，兼顾确定性与语义防护。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Security Operations Centers (SOCs) streamlines threat intelligence but introduces critical vulnerabilities, notably indirect prompt injection via log poisoning. Adversaries exploit this vector to execute multistep ``promptware'' kill chains by embedding malicious payloads within system logs to hijack the LLM's operational logic. Securing this pipeline presents a dichotomy: deterministic defenses are computationally efficient yet semantically blind, while purely neural evaluations introduce prohibitive latency and probabilistic flaws. To address this, we propose a novel neurosymbolic defense-in-depth architecture that ensures end-to-end pipeline integrity. The primary layer employs customized SIEM decoders as a deterministic pre-filter, performing immediate structural sanitization to neutralize volumetric padding and signature-based injections at the ingestion edge. The secondary layer leverages NeMo Guardrails to enforce strict semantic boundaries through self-checking validation on the structured SIEM alerts prior to LLM processing. Furthermore, the framework integrates a closed-loop telemetry system, providing critical Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) visibility into thwarted attacks directly within the SOC dashboard. We present a comprehensive experimental evaluation mapped to the MITRE ATLAS taxonomy, assessing the framework against diverse prompt injections. Our results demonstrate that this synergistic approach effectively dismantles the promptware kill chain - bounding LLM stochasticity with verifiable constraints, and delivering a resilient, highly observable defense mechanism for next-generation AI-SOCs.

</details>


### [164] [CARTS: Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography for Full-Capacity Keyed Text Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10744)
*Wissam Ghantous,Alexander V. Mantzaris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对上下文自回归秩转码隐写（CARTS）进行严格的形式化安全分析，定义相关安全概念与计算问题，并结合Llama 3 8B开展实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Norelli等人提出的Calgacus构造虽实验展示了利用自回归语言模型将载荷文本转换为等长隐写文本的现象，但缺乏形式化安全分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为CARTS建立严格的安全基础。

Method: 在确定性模型假设下证明CARTS的精确正确性；引入秩坐标表示，使密钥成为秩向量空间上的双射；定义上下文搜索、密钥碰撞、消息模糊度、编码映射非交换性等计算问题与安全概念，并研究其理论关系（如以上下文搜索刻画消息模糊度、密钥碰撞与消息模糊度的张力）；在Llama 3 8B上进行实证研究。

Result: 实验在所有测试案例中均确认原始载荷可被精确恢复；随机密钥生成下未发现密钥碰撞；证实人工构造的碰撞是局部而非全局的；未发现可交换密钥对，表明对所研究攻击向量具有抵抗力。

Conclusion: 本文为语言模型在密码学与隐私保护通信中的建设性应用开辟了有形式化基础的研究议程。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models can be used to transform a payload text into a stegotext of identical token length by preserving per-position rank information across contexts - a methodology we formalize as Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography (CARTS). While the Calgacus construction of Norelli et al. demonstrated this phenomenon experimentally, no formal security analysis existed. This paper provides the first rigorous treatment of CARTS. We show its exact correctness under deterministic model assumptions, introduce a rank-coordinate representation in which keys act as bijections on rank-vector space, define relevant security notions and the computational problems naturally associated with the construction - context search, key collisions, message equivocation, and non-commutativity of the encoding maps - and study the theoretical relationships between them, including the characterization of message equivocation in terms of context search, and the tension between key collisions and message equivocation. An empirical study on Llama 3 8B confirms exact recovery of the original payload in all tested cases, finds no key collisions under random key generation, establishes that a hand-crafted collision is local rather than global, and finds no commuting key pairs - suggesting resistance to the attack vectors studied. This work opens a formally grounded research agenda for the constructive use of language models in cryptography and privacy-preserving communication.

</details>


### [165] [Temporal and Multimodal Deep Learning for Cyberattack Detection in LEO Satellite Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10746)
*Kyle Stein,Guillermo Francia,Eman El-Sheikh,Hossain Shahriar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于卫星专用数据集UNSW-IoTSAT，系统研究深度学习卫星网络攻击检测，提出子系统融合MLP与分层多模态Transformer两种结构化架构，并采用抗泄漏的评估协议，其中分层Transformer在抗泄漏设置下达到91.66%准确率和85.63%宏F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星通信系统应用日益广泛，其射频链路、星载硬件与轨道运行产生异构信息，对智能攻击检测提出新需求。现有方法多依赖地面网络入侵数据集，或对样本独立评估，难以捕捉低轨卫星特有的时序攻击行为，且评估协议存在信息泄漏风险，导致结论可靠性不足。

Method: 使用卫星专用的UNSW-IoTSAT数据集开展深度学习攻击检测研究；设计保留硬件、轨道与射频信息的结构化学习架构，包括子系统融合MLP与分层多模态Transformer（建模跨子系统交互与时序演化）；同时构建抗泄漏的行级与时间级评估设置，并考察跨卫星泛化能力，分析模型架构与评估协议对检测性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明结构化多模态建模与严格评估具有显著价值；分层Transformer在抗泄漏评估协议下最高取得91.66%的准确率与85.63%的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: 面向低轨卫星的网络攻击检测应充分利用硬件、轨道与射频等多子系统的结构化多模态信息，并采用抗泄漏、跨卫星泛化的严谨评估协议，才能真实刻画模型的检测能力。

Abstract: The growing reliance on Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite communication systems has increased the need for intelligent methods capable of detecting cyberattacks across complex and dynamic space environments. Unlike conventional network intrusion detection, satellite systems generate heterogeneous information across radio-frequency (RF) links, onboard hardware, and orbital operations. However, many existing approaches either rely on terrestrial intrusion datasets or evaluate individual observations independently, limiting their ability to capture temporal attack behavior specific to LEO satellites. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of deep-learning-based cyberattack detection using the recently introduced satellite-specific UNSW-IoTSAT dataset. We investigate structured learning architectures that preserve hardware, orbital, and RF information, including a Subsystem-Fusion MLP and a hierarchical multimodal Transformer that models both cross-subsystem interactions and temporal evolution. We further evaluate leakage-resistant row-level and temporal settings, along with cross-satellite generalization, to characterize how model architecture and evaluation protocol influence satellite cyberattack detection. Experimental results demonstrate the value of structured multimodal modeling and rigorous evaluation, with the hierarchical Transformer achieving up to 91.66% accuracy and 85.63% macro F1 under the leakage-resistant evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Static Guarantees: Measuring the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail Gap in Security-Sensitive and LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10762)
*Jessica Pourleyli,Maitreyee Das Urmi,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail (SPDF) 现象，并设计三阶段智能体流水线（静态扫描 + LLM 驱动的 CWE 推理 + Docker 隔离环境中的自动化漏洞利用验证），在 1,355 个 Python 样本上验证：654 个通过 Bandit-Semgrep 复合静态门禁的样本中，95 个经动态验证确认或部分确认可被利用，综合流水线比率为 14.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析被广泛用作可扩展、可复现、低成本的安全门禁，但无法直接观察运行时利用行为。依赖对抗性输入、执行上下文或利用链的漏洞可能绕过静态检查却在实际中仍可被利用；然而通过静态分析常被误当作安全行为的证据（即"静态通过=安全"的错误假设）。作者旨在揭示并量化这一落差。

Method: 提出 SPDF 现象并构建三阶段智能体流水线：(1) 静态扫描（Bandit + Semgrep 复合门禁）；(2) LLM 驱动的 CWE 推理，识别候选漏洞；(3) 在隔离 Docker 容器中开展自动化漏洞利用验证。在 SecurityEval、RedCode、CyberNative 三个数据集的 1,355 个 Python 样本上进行评估。

Result: 654 个在复合 Bandit-Semgrep 门禁下无告警的样本中，LLM 检测阶段在 235 个文件中识别出 394 个候选漏洞；动态验证在 95 个文件中确认或部分确认可利用性，综合流水线比率为 14.53%（约每 7 个静态干净样本中 1 个）。按数据集划分，候选 file–CWE 对的确认为：RedCode 33.7%、CyberNative 28.6%、SecurityEval 5.4%。若干高频确认类别（如 CWE-338、CWE-916）未被 Bandit 和 Semgrep 任一工具标记。

Conclusion: 静态分析成功与运行时安全并非可互换的度量，而是软件保障的层级关系；静态通过不能作为安全行为的充分证据。该结论有望重塑 AI 生成代码及安全敏感代码的评估方式。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) fuel the quest for scalable methods to assess the security of generated and security-sensitive software. Static analysis is widely adopted as a scalable, reproducible, and inexpensive security gate, but cannot directly observe runtime exploit behaviour. Vulnerabilities dependent on adversarial inputs, execution context, or exploit chaining may evade static checks while remaining exploitable in practice, yet passing static analysis is often treated as evidence of secure behaviour. This paper introduces the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail (SPDF) phenomenon and a three-stage agentic pipeline combining static scanning, LLM-driven Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) reasoning, and autonomous exploit verification in isolated Docker containers. We evaluate 1,355 Python samples from SecurityEval, RedCode, and CyberNative datasets. Of the 654 samples producing no findings under the composite Bandit-Semgrep gate, the LLM detection stage identified 394 candidate vulnerabilities across 235 files. Dynamic verification confirmed or partially confirmed exploitability in 95 files, yielding an inclusive pipeline rate of 14.53% (roughly 1 in 7 statically clean samples). This rate represents the proportion of Bandit-Semgrep-clean samples for which the pipeline identified a candidate vulnerability and obtained runtime evidence supporting exploitability. Outcomes varied by dataset: among candidate file--CWE pairs, confirmed exploitability was 33.7% for RedCode, 28.6% for CyberNative, and 5.4% for SecurityEval. Several frequently confirmed classes, including CWE-338 and CWE-916, were flagged by neither Bandit nor Semgrep. These findings indicate that static-analysis success and runtime security are hierarchical layers of software assurance rather than interchangeable measures, and have the potential to reshape how AI-generated and security-sensitive code is evaluated.

</details>


### [167] [Big Enough to Break Out: Tracking the Rising Capability of LLM Penetration-Testing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10780)
*Victoria Lovelace,Cameron Berryman,Yuhan You,Suhas Reddy Adavelly,Joel Sadler,Daniel Graham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文比较了两种基于 PentestGPT 的渗透测试智能体系统（旧版人机协同 + Kimi K2.5，新版自主 + Claude Opus 4.8），在三个公开目标上新版全部解决，旧版即使失败也能完成约一半子任务；作者进一步加入"覆盖记忆层"检验长时程记忆假说，发现无改善，瓶颈更可能在规划与承诺能力而非记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正被越来越多地用于渗透测试，但对其实际能力边界与失败机制缺乏系统认识；同时，领域内普遍将长时程任务瓶颈归因于"长时程记忆丢失"，这一假设本身缺乏实证检验。作者希望厘清能力来源的归因问题，并检验该主流假设是否成立。

Method: 采用对比实验：在同一测试框架（PentestGPT）下比较两种配置——旧版 human-in-the-loop、开源权重 Kimi K2.5（运行于普通大学 GPU、无厂商护栏）与新版自主式 Claude Opus 4.8；在三个公开目标上评估完整任务成功率与子任务完成度评分，并对旧版停滞运行进行过程回放分析。随后在两个系统中均加入 coverage-memory 层，做干预性消融实验以检验记忆假说。

Result: 自主系统三个目标全部攻克，包含旧版始终未能完成的两个目标；旧版即便在失败机器上也完成约一半子任务。加入覆盖记忆层后，两个系统的最终结果均未改善。对旧版停滞过程的可审查部分显示，限制因素是规划与承诺（证据未能转化为具体的利用假设），而非记忆丢失。子任务评分可作为可量化的能力度量指标。

Conclusion: 由于模型、harness、自主性与记忆架构同时变化，作者只能描述趋势而无法给出因果解释；方向性提示是：进攻能力的进步更可能取决于智能体的规划与承诺能力，而非更好的记忆。同一套子任务评分也可供防御方使用，从而在能力上升过程中就进行度量与监测，而不是在实战中被动遭遇。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to penetration testing, but we still know little about what they can do or how they fail. We compare two PentestGPT-based systems: a legacy human-in-the-loop system running the open-weight Kimi K2.5, and a newer autonomous system running Claude Opus 4.8. Across three public targets, the autonomous system solves all three, including the two the legacy system never finishes. The legacy result is the more surprising of the two. Even on the machines the legacy system fails to solve, it completes about half the subtasks, while running on ordinary university GPUs with no provider guardrails. We can describe the trend but not explain it, since model, harness, autonomy, and memory architecture all change together. Its direction still points to the next question: what will limit these agents as they take on more complex tasks? The usual answer is long-horizon memory, the loss of access to earlier findings during long attack chains. We test it by adding a coverage-memory layer to both systems, and neither improves outcomes. In the legacy stalled runs we could review, the limiting factor appeared to be planning and commitment rather than lost memory: agents held the evidence for a route forward and never turned it into a concrete exploitation hypothesis, which may suggest that offensive capability will advance with agents' ability to plan rather than with better memory. The same subtask scoring that tracks this capability is available to defenders, who can measure it as it rises instead of waiting to meet it in the field.

</details>


### [168] [No-Box Vulnerability Analysis: Description-only Detection of Indirect Prompt Injection Vulnerabilities in MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10854)
